No universal facial formula selected, no reopening of unappealed findings, no claims about later drug admission. |

## Framework joinder ceiling and assembly handoff

These are **provisional commitments**, not assignments or published opinion topology. Blackmun will join protected choice, viability, health, actual coercion/obstacle prohibition and inadequate-fit invalidations as minimum protections, but refuses replacement of his Roe/trimester scrutiny with a weaker exclusive formula. Stevens will join a concrete framework preserving choice/viability/health, independently prohibiting substantial obstacles to protected care, testing meaningful lesser burdens for permissible justification and fit, and putting that justification on the State once a meaningful burden is shown; he refuses treating the State's wish to discourage the protected choice alone as sufficient justification, ignoring cumulative/affected-woman burdens, or importing a minor-consultation exception into adult delay. Souter joins core preservation and obstacle prohibition as a floor, rejects immunity for every lesser burden, but has no frozen commitment to either a new comprehensive threshold-only or a new comprehensive two-tier rule; exact propositions and any revision require reconciliation rather than an invented preexisting abortion position.

For all three, proof of a serious burden on a relevant class cannot be answered simply by counting women whom the provision does not affect. None adopts an unspecified numerical facial denominator. All require every statutory protection to retain its actual terms, distinguish findings from commands, preserve physician judgment in the supplied risk-based emergency construction, and distinguish public facility reports from confidential individual records. No historical lineup or assignment supplies any row. Comparator review must now confirm each component or record a reasoned departure; only afterward may Stone's actual Section II be opened and an opinion-by-opinion join test performed.

## Pre-global-freeze scope refresh and explicit additional commitments

The neutral packet's appended **Neutral scope addendum — spousal compelled speech and emergency vagueness** was personally source-validated and incorporated before any comparator opening. The omission concerned the scope catalog, not new evidence or a changed substantive conclusion. This refreeze adds separate ground/reach rows and does not silently rewrite the prior rows.

| Justice/component; own/public sources | Two paths and strongest counterarguments | Refrozen ground, reach and join boundary |
|---|---|---|
| Blackmun — spousal compelled speech; BT/BO; L/R | A compelled intimate notification may independently implicate expressive autonomy, and Tr.51–52 expressly presents that claim. B decide the established abortion-liberty ground and avoid a distinct speech holding whose full preservation and standard need separate resolution. Strongest A argument is communication forced under sanctions; strongest B is the liberty ruling already removes the entire notice prerequisite without equating all notices with ideological affirmations. | Select B: invalidate notice on liberty/record grounds, leave independent spousal First Amendment claim and its preservation undecided as unnecessary. Refuses a finding that the claim was never raised, a holding that spouses' communications are commercial speech, or a claim this is a separately controlling speech invalidation. Minimum revision for a joined opinion is to state that express reservation; an affirmative speech holding requires a new actual-standard join test. |
| Stevens — spousal compelled speech; SH/ST/SR; L | A SH protects family communication from state-standardized intrusion and SR rejects automatic commercial classification of compelled communication. B avoid extending those sources into a new independent spousal speech test when liberty/private-family reasoning supplies complete relief. Strongest A point is the text's compelled-notice condition and express argument; strongest B is distinction between notice of a fact and compelled ideological allegiance. | Select B; no independent First Amendment merits holding or waiver determination. Can join acknowledgement of the claim and reservation, not a description that it vanished or was conceded. A separate speech rationale would require identifying the actual compelled act, review standard, relevant exceptions and preservation basis. |
| Souter — spousal compelled speech; SP; L/C/W | A actual compulsion of intimate communication raises a serious independent constitutional question; B precise source and threshold discipline counsel deciding the preserved, sufficient liberty claim first. Strongest A point is State-conditioned disclosure under sanctions, not mere private disagreement; strongest B is that general method and argument questions are no date-eligible abortion/speech holding. | Select B: liberty invalidation gives complete relief, so independent spousal speech/preservation are left open. No invented Souter position on whether every mandated family notice violates the First Amendment. Cannot join a categorical rejection based on a commercial-speech label or a claim no theory was presented. |
| Blackmun — standalone emergency vagueness; BT/BA; R | A clinical sanctions and nonauthoritative construction can create a constitutional notice defect independent of health coverage. B health/construction is the developed question, whereas distinct vagueness preservation is contested and no further ground is needed here. Strongest A point is BA's warning about physician uncertainty; strongest B is express prospective good-faith risk language and limited presented merits. | Select B: sustain the facial health/construction disposition only; no independent holding rejecting vagueness and no agreed waiver. The objection remains available if properly presented, especially after a contrary authoritative state construction. Cannot join an assertion that statutory clarity was separately adjudicated. |
| Stevens — standalone emergency vagueness; SW; C/R | A SW's text discipline and physician sanctions support a separate clarity inquiry; B adjudicating a not-separately-developed theory would exceed what the current health/construction ruling requires. Strongest A point is uncertainty cannot be cured by judicial wish; strongest B is the express prospective risk wording and disputed preservation. | Select B: leave independent vagueness and preservation unresolved; uphold only under the stated health-protecting construction. No broad pronouncement that the statute is clear in every application. |
| Souter — standalone emergency vagueness; SP; W/C/M | A medicine-plus-sanctions calls for an administrable notice standard; B threshold discipline and a sufficient narrower disposition favor reserving the contested independent claim. Strongest A point is actual deterrence of lawful care; strongest B is that calling text vague in a factual section is not necessarily a preserved standalone merits argument. | Select B: decide health/construction, reserve standalone vagueness and waiver. Cannot treat uncertainty about preservation as proof of waiver or a new merits rejection. |

**Parent disposition precision:** All three vote to sustain §3206 against the present facial attack without treating district finding232 as an enacted clinic-visit command. Their reservation concerning an actual compelled visit means that ordinary proceedings/application challenges remain possible; it does **not** require a separate special remand to adjudicate a hypothetical visit command or invalidate the entire informed-parent provision. A disposition that affirmatively holds bypass necessarily cures every needless visit remains outside all three commitments. The ordinary mandate implementing the provision-by-provision judgment is unaffected.

**Refreeze status:** The foregoing is the final provisional handoff before the global comparator gate. No historical comparison or Stone text was used in the added rows or the disposition clarification.

### Final parent-content and alternative-ground precision before global freeze

The parent's informed-consent duty is §3206(a)'s text, but the statute does **not** separately define it or expressly incorporate each §3205 delivery mechanism. The district court's pp.1382–1384 analysis used §3205 to define that content and invalidated §3206 additionally on emergency/content grounds. This is a **lower-court construction**, distinct from the statute and the clinic-attendance finding. The following rows resolve the content question separately; they do not add a clinic-visit command or delete the word informed wholesale.

| Justice; own/public source | Opposed paths and strongest arguments | Exact provisional disposition and joinder |
|---|---|---|
| Blackmun — parental informed content; BT/BA/BO; R | A the ancillary information invalid when compulsorily given to the minor cannot become a mandatory precondition by transmitting it to her parent; B parental deliberation gives the same accurate option information added relevance, even when the woman could decide without it. Strongest B argument is the parent's protective role; strongest A response is that protection concerns the minor's best interests, not State-imposed persuasion, and a consent route cannot reintroduce invalid prerequisites. | Sustain ordinary informed parental consent, including medically relevant nature/risks/alternatives, with elective maturity/best-interest bypass. Invalidate incorporation of the mandatory §3205(a)(2)(i), (ii), and (iii) ancillary commands into the parent's required consent, to the same extent as the commands to the woman. Permit voluntary relevant ancillary counseling, optional materials and accurate certification of lawful receipt. **Affirm the Third Circuit's rejection of facial invalidation of §3206 as a whole, but reverse insofar as its judgment permits enforcement of those invalid incorporated ancillary prerequisites.** No special attendance remand; ordinary mandate implements severance. This is a distinct partial parental-content vote, not an unqualified vote to uphold every informed-parent condition. |
| Stevens — parental informed content; SH/SO/ST; R | A accurate medical and limited financial/material-availability information reasonably supports the parent's best-interest advice; B forcing the same information can burden an already informed parent and child. Strongest B argument is unnecessary repetition; strongest A is the useful parental function, lawful qualified delivery, optional ultimate receipt and lack of an independent prescribed clinic visit. | Sustain the ordinary medical and the narrowly construed accurate ancillary content when used to obtain informed parental consent, with the actual emergency, incest/unavailability and bypass protections. **Affirm the Third Circuit's §3206 facial disposition; no vacatur and no special parental-attendance remand.** Do not imply approval of a mandatory personal clinic appearance, a new nondelegable parental-counseling duty, or a parent-specific waiting period absent an established construction. The woman's invalid fixed24hour and physician-only commands remain invalid regardless of parental participation. |
| Souter — parental informed content; SP; M/R/C | A established informed parental participation can use truthful relevant medical/options information; B §3205's detailed mechanisms should not automatically define every parent's consent or require an unnecessary trip. Strongest B argument is precise text and actual burdens; strongest A is narrow consent information without inventing implementation. | Sustain informed-parent medical and accurate ancillary content at that limited level. **Affirm the Third Circuit's §3206 facial disposition; no vacatur and no special attendance remand.** Reserve an actual enforced visit, special parental wait or personal-delivery construction not established here. The woman's fixed delay and physician monopoly are separately severed. |

**Alternative-ground holding count for each of Blackmun, Stevens and Souter:** No independently sufficient equal-protection holding is selected, either narrow or broad. No independently sufficient general marital-privacy or informational-privacy holding is selected, either narrow or broad. Private decisionmaking, bodily autonomy, absence of a spouse's controlling authority, the actual coercive effects of mandated disclosure, and the relevant woman's ability to exercise protected choice remain parts of the **abortion-liberty rationale**, not a second freestanding constitutional ground. For Stevens, SH's private-family reasoning informs that application; it does not automatically add an independently sufficient holding. All three expressly decline the distinct spousal First Amendment ground and its preservation question as unnecessary after full notice invalidation. Thus none may be counted as silently joining an independent equality, general-privacy or speech holding merely because the disposition or descriptive concerns coincide.

**All three attendance judgments, without alternatives:** No additional remand devoted to statutory attendance construction; no vacatur of §3206 on the supposed presence of an enacted attendance command. All affirm rejection of a facial challenge to §3206 based on that supposed command. They preserve the question only for a properly presented actual construction/application and refuse the universal claim that bypass cures every needless visit. Blackmun's separately specified ancillary-content severance is the sole partial reversal of the parental informed-content disposition in these three rows.

### Actual modeling-clarification log

Before the global freeze and before any comparator or Stone opening, source-catalog review exposed the need for distinct spousal-speech and standalone emergency-vagueness reach rows; the neutral addendum and six rows above were added after checking the actual transcript and provider report. Root's completeness review then requested exact parent judgments and alternative-ground scope. The provisional expressions suggesting a possible attendance remand were normalized to affirmance of the facial attendance ruling without a special remand; Blackmun's invalid ancillary-content incorporation was separately specified, and the absence of independently sufficient equality/general-privacy holdings was made explicit in each original row. A reporter pinpoint range not independently verified for SP was removed; the exact full own writing and case citation remain. These are actual source/clarity refinements during modeling, not invented judicial circulation, historical-comparator departures, or post-Stone movements. The resulting original rows and addenda are the single final provisional position for each component.

# Provisional commitments — White, Scalia, Thomas

**Casey, Nos. 91-744 and 91-902; June 29, 1992.** Mode: single-conversation fallback. Neutral framing was frozen in `neutral-modeling-packet.md` before these commitments were formed. The modeler read that whole packet, the specified full own writings and the public continuity identified below. No Stone supplement, comparator, historical Casey opinions, prior Casey record, or prior Casey commitment was opened. Incidental historical search/IA metadata exposure during source retrieval was previously disclosed; true blindness is unavailable. This is a new issue-specific model, not adoption of a prior commitment. It proposes neither Court-wide result nor authorship.

**Freeze status:** These are the complete provisional commitments supplied for the eight-Justice freeze. A comparator may confirm them or disclose a departure, but cannot supply them. Any post-Stone movement requires an Engine §8 reconciliation audit. A modeler's decisional alternative is not an invented historical vote, dialogue, draft, or concession.

## Sources and reading gate

Full own-writing reads: White's Doe/Roe dissent, 410 U.S. 221–223; Danforth concurrence/dissent, 428 U.S. 92–101; Thornburgh dissent, 476 U.S. 786–814; Brennan's Michael M. dissent joined by White, 450 U.S. 488–496; Riley Court opinion joined by White and, except footnote 11, Scalia, 487 U.S. 781–803; Scalia's Riley concurrence, 803–804; Scalia's Webster concurrence, 492 U.S. 532–537; Scalia's Ohio v. Akron Center concurrence, 497 U.S. 520–521. Raw primary files and extraction trace are in `white_scalia_thomas_authority_notes.md`. These writings were read in full, including material notes; selected lines were not treated as substitutes for the whole writing.

Public continuity checked in `output/`: **C5-Collins** (February 26, chunk 5), **C5-Hudson** (February 25, chunk 5), **C9-Foucha** (May 18, chunk 9), **C9-Riggins** (May 18, chunk 9), **C9-Quill** (May 26, chunk 9), **C12-McCollum** (June 18, chunk 12), **C12-Wright** (June 19, chunk 12), **C13-Medina** (June 22, chunk 13), **C13-Lee** (June 24, chunk 13); White's **C3-Hilton** (December 16, chunk 3). Those short citations below identify the exact case/entry, not a generic term-wide tendency. The neutral packet supplies the relevant authors, joins and limits. No Thomas inference rests on Hilton, in which he did not participate. Thomas has no date-eligible abortion writing; his rows explicitly identify the unresolved methodological question and its bounded modeled resolution.

Citation keys: **W-Roe** = White's Doe/Roe dissent; **W-Danforth** = White's Danforth separate writing; **W-Thornburgh** = White's Thornburgh dissent; **W-MichaelM** = his Michael M. dissent join; **Riley-W** = White's full Court-opinion join; **S-Webster**, **S-Ohio**, **S-Riley** = Scalia's own writings just identified, with Riley including his full Court join except footnote 11. Each table row's final field cites both a date-eligible own-writing source and actual simulated public continuity. Thomas's public writings are his available own writings; no abortion authority is falsely attributed to him.

The statutory baseline is the full verbatim statutory appendix and supplemental source quotations in the frozen neutral packet. It is incorporated as the source of each exact provision, not a paraphrase of the prior Supreme Court result. The qualifications stated in the component entries below are statutory unless expressly called findings or constructions. The proposed judgments concern the challenged applications and provisions only, not unappealed items. Sustaining a facially challenged provision does not establish that every future false publication, confidentiality breach or coercive enforcement application is lawful.

## White

Each row gives **Path A**, **Path B / strongest counterargument**, **commitment**, **join barrier / minimum revision**, **narrower available position**, and **own-writing / public sources**. Path B is genuinely supported by a source or record premise; it is not an unattributed ideological alternative.

| Component | Two supported paths and strongest counterargument | Frozen commitment and ground | Join barrier, minimum revision and narrower position | Sources |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Constitutional protected choice | A: follow W-Roe and W-Thornburgh: abortion is not a fundamental constitutional entitlement; ordinary liberty does not settle priority over fetal life. B: retain Roe because settled bodily liberty and repeated precedent have practical force; White's Foucha/Riggins commitments show constitutional liberty is real. Strongest objection to A: pregnancy is an affirmative state restriction on the body, unlike Collins's omission. | Would overrule Roe's fundamental abortion-right foundation and employ rational review of the challenged regulations, while independently considering speech, equality and privacy. He does not say there is no constitutional liberty or that every medical-necessity prohibition is valid. | Cannot join constitutional protection of elective previability choice. Minimum revision removes that proposition and identifies an independent source for any surviving limit. Narrower route: sustain regulations under existing cases where consistent, but he would separately state Roe should be overruled. | W-Roe; W-Thornburgh; C9-Foucha; C9-Riggins; C5-Collins. |
| Viability, trimester machinery and State's potential-life interest | A: State's interest does not originate at viability; W-Thornburgh treats fetal life as compelling throughout. B: preserve previability choice while allowing reasonable earlier regulation, separating implementation from foundation as Quill separated constitutional grounds. Strongest B point: reforming implementation need not reject protected liberty. | Rejects both trimester restrictions and viability as constitutional limit on protection of fetal life. Does not decide every postviability restriction or life/health application. | Cannot join replacement machinery that leaves previability prohibition categorically forbidden. Minimum revision makes fetal-life regulation subject to independently applicable constitutional limits without that cutoff. Narrower: approve particular challenged laws without settling all possible bans. | W-Thornburgh; W-Roe's express medical-necessity reservation; C9-Quill. |
| Stare decisis | A: W-Thornburgh expressly permits correction of constitutional error without novel arguments; Roe's basis and line drawing warrant correction. B: White joined Hilton's separable reliance protection and existing liberty in Foucha; reproductive expectations are substantial. Strongest B objection: overruling cannot rest on turnover or continued dissent alone. | Favors overruling Roe after confronting its substantive foundation and practical effects, not because membership changed. Constitutional self-government and absence of a defensible source outweigh adherence here; he does not deny all reliance. | Cannot join a declaration that disagreement with Roe is insufficient absent a newly changed factual premise. Minimum revision permits fundamental constitutional error as part of a reasoned overruling justification. Narrower: retain particular nondependent doctrines and exact rules independently justified. | W-Thornburgh 786–800; C3-Hilton; C9-Quill (Commerce dissent and due-process join). |
| An independently impermissible substantial obstacle | A: without the abortion entitlement, severe access effect does not itself invalidate legislation with rational fetal-life basis. B: if Roe governs, effective prevention differs from peripheral regulation; W-Thornburgh accepts that protected conduct cannot be deliberately harassed. Strongest B point: the full trial findings can establish a real obstacle. | Rejects a free-standing substantial-obstacle prohibition tied to previability abortion. Would analyze an independently protected interest on its own law; will not deny that the record contains serious burdens. | Cannot join any rule making severity alone dispositive of constitutionality. Minimum revision identifies a constitutional interest and its actual governing test. Narrower: assume Roe only for an alternative application; do not make the assumed test his own framework. | W-Thornburgh (fundamental-right and harassment discussions); C9-Foucha; C13-Medina. |

B: White's Riggins join requires justification and consideration of less intrusive means for recognized bodily liberty; Michael M. demands actual sex-classification fit. Strongest B point: smaller burdens can violate those sources. | Rejects a general abortion rule invalidating meaningful burdens whenever judicially assessed justification or fit is inadequate. Accepts genuine fit scrutiny where an independently established constitutional source requires it. | Cannot join general least-restrictive-means or balancing language for abortion. Minimum revision confines heightened fit to the relevant independent right. Narrower: fact-specific rejection of deception or targeted harassment under those sources. | W-Thornburgh; W-MichaelM; C9-Riggins; C13-Medina. |
| Burden of justification once meaningful burden shown | A: W-Danforth rejects shifting constitutional-validity proof to the legislature on assumed burdens; ordinary review leaves challengers to establish invalidity. Strongest B point: correct burden depends on source, not a presumption favoring every law. | No automatic State persuasion burden from meaningful abortion burden alone. The State carries the burden where speech, sex equality or a recognized involuntary deprivation specifically requires it. | Cannot join a universal abortion burden shift. Minimum revision identifies the independent source and its trigger. | Purpose, cumulative effects and relevant population | A: legislature may promote childbirth and deliberation; effects on actual regulated people must be considered, but no numerical denominator supplies a universal test. B: selective coercion of a vulnerable group cannot be defeated by counting unaffected people; White's Foucha insists on a real authorized predicate. Strongest B point: aggregate barriers may make a formally modest command consequential. | Accepts full findings and evaluates each provision and supported combined effect; rejects a special abortion denominator rule or automatic invalidity from discouragement. Rationality and independent rights remain the selected legal measures. | Cannot join language treating persuasion toward childbirth as itself illicit or turning all cumulative burdens into a constitutional claim. Minimum revision separates lawful persuasion from independently forbidden coercion. | Medical emergency §3203 and emergency explanation §3205(b) | A: sustain prospective good-faith clinical judgment: immediate abortion to avert death or delay creating serious risk of substantial and irreversible impairment of a major bodily function; explanation before the abortion only if possible. B: the message may increase anxiety, be difficult to enforce, or communicate unwanted persuasion. ### White's spousal-exception precision and conclusion

His sustained facial rule uses §3209's actual text: a signed, nonnotarized notice statement; alternatively a signed, nonnotarized statement that spouse is not father, cannot be located despite diligent effort, pregnancy results from §3128 spousal sexual assault reported to law enforcement with jurisdiction, or she has reason to believe notice likely will cause bodily injury to her by spouse or another. Both statement forms warn false statements are punishable. The medical-emergency exception is §3203's good-faith clinical judgment of immediate abortion to avert death or delay creating serious risk of substantial and irreversible major-function impairment. District finding297 identifies the incorporated assault law's90-day reporting restriction; this is a finding about that law, distinct from the rape permission in §3205(a)(2)(iii). There is no general psychological/economic/child-injury/divorce/custody exception. White does not invent one; his facial conclusion instead disputes the legal consequence of the full found burdens under the sources he accepts. His Danforth position is the primary source; independent equality and privacy objections have been confronted, not absorbed into an abortion label.

## Scalia

Additional own source read fully before these commitments: **S-MichaelH**, Scalia's plurality in Michael H. v. Gerald D.,491U.S.110, including footnote6 and the separate equal-protection/preservation discussion. This supplies Scalia's method and actual limited familial-right reasoning; its result is not an abortion precedent. Footnote6 has only Scalia/Rehnquist and is not attributed to O'Connor or Kennedy. His account expressly distinguishes the fact that liberty extends beyond physical restraint from the particular asserted entitlement's constitutional foundation.

For every row, Path B states the strongest counterargument to Path A. The commitment column states the response to Path B; where a narrower route could avoid choosing, it is named in the next column. Thus neither pathway is presented without an adversarial test.

### Scalia's spousal-exception precision

The §3209 notice and substitute-exception statements are signed and need not be notarized, with false-statement warnings. Substitute certification is available if spouse is not father; diligent effort cannot locate him; pregnancy results from §3128 spousal assault reported to the competent law-enforcement agency; or the woman has reason to believe notice likely will cause bodily injury to her by spouse or another. Section3203's clinical medical emergency independently excuses notice: immediate abortion to avert death or delay creating serious risk of substantial irreversible major-function impairment. District finding297 supplies the incorporated assault law's90-day restriction; neither its reporting requirement nor timing is attached to §3205(a)(2)(iii)'s rape-information permission. Psychological/economic harm, threats to children, divorce/custody retaliation and publicity are not general exceptions. Scalia's rejection of facial invalidity accepts their existence in the record and disputes their legal consequence under his source-specific framework; it does not fabricate statutory protection for them.

## Thomas

**No abortion-writing premise.** Thomas has no date-eligible abortion opinion. His own simulated writings, read from the public record, provide the primary evidence: Hudson and Foucha dissents, Riggins dissent (including his own separate scope objection, which Scalia did not join), McCollum judgment concurrence, Wright principal opinion, and his actual joins in Quill, Medina, Lee and Collins. Those sources establish neither a concealed abortion view nor a preexisting undue-burden formula.

**Baseline versus fresh choice.** Roe and governing progeny enter as precedent; Thomas's failure to have written on abortion does not suspend their force. One supported pathway is exact adherence, reflected in his McCollum application of Edmonson despite disagreement and his Quill retention of the specific Commerce rule. A different pathway, available because the parties squarely present reconsideration here, is to reject the constitutional foundation after comparing text, tradition, institutional limits, workability and reliance. His Quill join also accepts overruling a constitutional due-process rule, demonstrating that adherence is not absolute. The provisional model chooses the second pathway for the Roe foundation, while keeping each independent constitutional claim separate. This is a new modeled conclusion at this event, not a prior position and not a result imported from history.

**Unresolved question and bounded conclusion.** The principal uncertainty is whether Thomas would regard the weight of settled reproductive expectations as sufficient to retain Roe despite his source/history concerns. No own abortion writing resolves it. The bounded conclusion here is that, on the present squarely briefed reconsideration and his source-specific earlier methods, he would decline to recognize elective previability abortion as a fundamental constitutional entitlement and would sustain these regulations under rational review unless a separate constitutional source requires more. He does not deny all substantive liberty, all reliance, all privacy, all sex equality, or all protection for medically necessary care. Component-specific uncertainties below narrow that conclusion; they are not filled by another Justice's or the historical Court's outcome.

For every row Path B supplies the strongest challenge to Path A, and the chosen ground answers Path B at the bounded scope. An unresolved model question is expressly identified rather than represented as a known past Thomas view.

### Thomas's exact spousal qualifications

Section3209 requires a signed, nonnotarized statement of notice, warning that false statements are punishable. A signed, nonnotarized exception statement may instead certify that spouse is not father; cannot be located despite diligent effort; pregnancy results from§3128 spousal assault reported to law enforcement with jurisdiction; or she has reason to believe notice likely will cause bodily injury to her by spouse or another. It too carries the false-statement warning. Medical emergency independently excuses notice under§3203's good-faith clinical judgment: immediate abortion to avert death or delay creating serious risk of substantial irreversible major-function impairment. District finding297 supplies the incorporated assault law's90-day reporting restriction. Section3205(a)(2)(iii)'s rape-information permission has no police-report condition and is not a direction to omit. The statute does not create general exemptions for psychological/economic coercion, threatened harm to children, divorce/custody retaliation or publicity. Thomas's bounded facial sustention accepts that deficiency's factual consequences and asks which independent constitutional source supplies relief; it does not assume a nonexistent exception or equate the absence of formal veto with absence of harm.

## Cross-row assembly controls and final handoff

1. All three Justices' **primary provision judgments** sustain the challenged appealed nonspousal provisions and would reverse the Third Circuit's spousal invalidation. That matching disposition is not a common complete opinion or automatic joinder. Their source, precedent, facial-scope and independent-right grounds differ and require exact formulation testing.
2. None would join affirmative constitutional elective-previability choice, a viability cutoff, a stand-alone substantial-obstacle prohibition, a general lesser-meaningful-burden/deficient-fit rule, or an automatic State justification burden merely on showing meaningful abortion burden. All can join accurate description of the statute, medical/parental procedural safeguards, statement that independent speech/equality/privacy rights remain applicable, and scope/record limits, but only at the precise grounds their rows accept.
3. White's precedent choice is anchored in his own express abortion dissents; Scalia's in his own Webster/Ohio Akron writings and source criticism; Thomas's is a fresh bounded conclusion from general method and actual prior-term public positions, with his McCollum/Quill adherence counterpath unresolved as model uncertainty and explicitly confronted. They are not interchangeable.
4. **Conditional report remedy is not a switched vote.** If another coalition invalidates§3209, each has recognized that its enforcement rationale cannot itself justify the dependent notice/reason fields of§3214(a)(12). Recognition alone does not join invalidation, decide independent marital-status data, or count as a conditional concurrence. Assembly must present the actual severance/remaining-scope formulation, test it against each row, and separately record any conditional remedial join and basis. A changed substantive commitment requires§8 reconciliation, not silent counting.
5. **Guardian and unappealed scope:** no new independent merits ruling on the adult-incompetent guardian class; no reopening district-upheld unappealed ascertainment, most confidential collection or§3214(a)(5)'s gestational-age result. A discrete disclosure/public-release ruling cannot erase all collection.
6. **No broad vagueness holding:** proposed emergency sustention rests on prospective good-faith clinical construction and developed health claim. Record may describe Third Circuit's vagueness ruling and officials' waiver contention without converting them into a fresh independently sufficient Supreme Court vagueness holding.
7. **Adversarial-field convention:** in every row, Path A states its affirmative authority, Path B states the best supported rival and the strongest objection to A, and the selected-ground column explains why that rival does not prevail at the bounded scope. The join-barrier/narrower field records what can be revised without changing the commitment and what would instead require reconciliation. No possible alternative is represented as a past vote.
8. **Framework limits are part of every join test.** A shared 'undue burden' label or matching disposition cannot secure any join to a proposition these rows reject. Conversely, refusal of an abortion framework does not prevent joining an independently justified medical-information, confidentiality, proper-scope or severability proposition. Only actual drafted grounds can answer the later join question.

### Equality standard clarified before freeze

Additional full primary read: **Caban v. Mohammed,441U.S.380**, Powell's whole Court opinion including notes (28,464characters), in `authorities/caban.json`, extracted under `briefs/caban-court-*.txt`. White actually joined this Court opinion. Neither Scalia nor Thomas is attributed a join or an earlier Caban position. Caban states the controlling sex-classification formulation from Craig: an important governmental objective and a substantial relationship between classification and objective; it rejects universal parental-role generalizations and distinguishes actual relationships, preserving unpresented questions rather than deciding them by analogy.

- **White:** applies intermediate scrutiny to the actual husband-entitlement/wife-duty classification, consistent with W-MichaelM and his Caban join. The asserted objective is actual marital/paternal participation in a decision concerning their prospective child, not women's incapacity. He finds the limited notice requirement substantially related to that objective, distinguishing a legal veto and confronting the actual exceptions and abuse findings. The strongest contrary argument is that the compulsory rule burdens the very relationships where participation is coercive, so notice is not a sufficiently tailored protection of legitimate participation. His response is that intermediate scrutiny does not require an individualized relationship-quality adjudication before any factual notice, while the bodily-injury, nonpaternity, location, assault and medical-emergency provisions constrain the duty. This is his source-specific equality conclusion, not a finding that every harmful relationship is exempt or a rational-basis substitution.
- **Scalia:** **assumes without deciding that intermediate scrutiny applies**, rather than claiming pregnancy removes all sex classification. Applying the important-objective/substantial-relationship test, would reject the facial equality claim for the actual limited notice and parental-participation fit described in his row. He accepts that Caban forbids a blanket presumption mothers and fathers have categorically different interests; the present rationale recognizes the father's actual prospective-parent interest without granting control over the procedure. The strongest contrary argument is the unequal legal power and the coercive affected population, not merely different biology. He concludes that notice's information function and the statute's actual limiting conditions are sufficiently related to the important interest, without treating Michael H.'s child-status rationality test as the governing sex test. Whether this classification independently requires that tier is left undecided.
- **Thomas:** likewise **assumes without deciding intermediate scrutiny**, applying existing sex-equality precedent rather than inventing a Thomas abortion/equality writing. The bounded modeled conclusion is that a factual notice duty concerning the spouse's prospective child is substantially related to the important actual participation interest, with no legal veto or ideological assent. The strongest counterargument is that a husband-only right/wife-only duty enforces a relation of authority precisely where consensual dialogue is absent; his answer rests on the distinction between information and legal control plus actual statutory limits, not a claim no injury occurs. Remaining uncertainty is both the classification's proper tier and how his method would resolve a proved coercive application. His assumption supplies an answer at the governing heightened standard without resolving those broader questions.

### Emergency vagueness reach separately frozen

All three **leave a standalone notice/clarity vagueness claim and its preservation undecided**. The physicians' good-faith prospective clinical judgment and the federal saving construction resolve their present health/construction sustention; that does not generate an independently sufficient vagueness holding. Officials' failure-to-renew contention and the absence of a developed independent providers' vagueness section are sources for the reach reservation, not agreed waiver. Neither federal appellate construction nor the Commonwealth's litigating agreement is represented as a Pennsylvania state-court holding. Counterpath would decide vagueness if properly presented because clinical judgment constrains enforcement; strongest objection is unpredictable enforcement despite that language. The selected narrower path avoids a unnecessary broader holding while retaining the actual prospective health construction. White's source constraints are W-Thornburgh/C12-Wright; Scalia's S-Ohio/S-MichaelH/C12-Wright; Thomas's C9-Riggins/C12-Wright. No vote on an independently unadjudicated vagueness claim should appear in a merits majority count.

# Comparator reconciliation — O’Connor and Kennedy

**Stage3, after the global eight-Justice freeze and before Stone.** Root saved `provisional-all-eight.md` and `neutral-packet-at-global-freeze.md` before comparator access. This reviewer then read the exact runtime Casey comparator and **all134,197characters of the freshly retrieved CAP joint opinion**, including its complete statutory appendix, in ten contiguous14,000-character segments. No segment was truncated. Source: `historical-casey-opinion-0.txt`, from [505U.S.833](https://static.case.law/us/505/cases/0833-01.json). This review does not claim to have read the four other historical writings; root and the other assigned reviewers handle those. No Stone text or earlier simulated Casey decision/output was opened.

The comparison is against `provisional-oconnor-kennedy.md`, final pre-comparator hash `3c459eb0ff8be459276d03f069807c01810c9fa22637b1f84e091318a6bb378c`. Its authority keys OA,OT,OW,OH/OHS,OG,OM/KM,KW,KH,KO,KP,KJ and its exact earlier-public-source keys R,L,M,C,Q/H,X are incorporated as citations to the fully identified writings, not as substitutes for sources. The appendix and lower-court text remain the source of statutory terms and findings; historical judicial paraphrases do not amend them.

**Reconciliation result:** provision dispositions largely confirm both freezes. There are substantive departures in general framework, institutional stare-decisis reasoning and O’Connor’s independently sufficient equality/privacy grounds, together with a remedial difference concerning severability and marital-status reporting. These are not hidden as mere differences in labels. No commitment is changed by this comparison. The comparator is strong contrary behavioral evidence on the disputed general propositions; the Justice-specific reasons for retaining the freeze are stated below. A later circulated argument can still trigger the Engine’s required reconciliation if it actually moves a commitment. Neither historical joint authorship nor historical joins supplies any simulated author or coalition.

## Historical facts used, with authority-level limits

O’Connor, Kennedy and Souter jointly authored the historical opinion. The source identifies PartsI,II,III,V-A,V-C,VI as the Court’s opinion; Stevens additionally joined V-E, making that reporting writing four; IV,V-B,V-D were the trio’s writing. Thus a historical judgment majority sustaining a provision is not automatically five signatures for the trio’s rationale. The runtime comparator’s grouped summaries of counseling, parental and reporting votes must be disaggregated against the other historical writings before an all-Justice historical table is published. This review confirms the **individual O’Connor and Kennedy positions**, not every other Justice’s vote on every subitem.

Historical PartI, at846, describes retained protection for previability choice, the inability of State interests to support a prohibition or substantial obstacle before viability, postviability restriction with life/health protection, and legitimate maternal-health/potential-life interests from the outset. PartIV, at872–879, rejects trimester machinery and gives the general purpose-or-effect substantial-obstacle formulation. It expressly acknowledges inconsistent earlier uses, cites O’Connor’s earlier possibility of a justified undue burden, and rejects that possibility for the fetal-life regulation described. Its broad paragraph also addresses a substantial obstacle serving “some other valid state interest”; its summary separately speaks of **unnecessary** health regulation. Those distinctions must be retained. It is inaccurate either to call the whole historical exposition simply O’Connor’s unchanged Akron test or to omit its qualifier concerning unnecessary health regulation.

Historical medical emergency sustention appears at879–880; informed consent, medical/ancillary information, physician delivery and waiting at881–887; spousal notice at887–898; parental consent/bypass at899–900; reports at900–901; the judgment and severability remand at901. Independent counseling First Amendment objections receive a short merits rejection at884 based on factual disclosure within medical practice, with Wooley acknowledged. There is no separate spousal First Amendment holding or freestanding emergency-vagueness holding. Spousal reasoning uses protected liberty, bodily integrity, family privacy and rejection of marital hierarchy; it does **not** separately announce Hogan’s intermediate-scrutiny test as an independently sufficient equal-protection holding.

Historical elaboration confirms the selected ordinary rationale. | KP directly demands special justification beyond constitutional disagreement; KW identifies a defect in machinery but no need to repudiate the core; H/Q show component-sensitive adherence. Historical III-A confirms these reasons. |
| Extra precedential force for divisive watershed decisions/overruling under fire | **Substantive reason/joinder departure.** Historical III-B/C expressly adds institutional-legitimacy reasoning and rare heightened force for such decisions; both historical Justices adopted it. | The freeze declined an independent controversy/legitimacy rule. OA/OT were willing to correct abortion precedents despite controversy; OW demands necessity, not a special category defined by public opposition. Q/H use actual rule/reliance analysis. Historical III-C strongly challenges that narrowness but is not a missing neutral fact or a dated prior commitment. | KP requires special justification generally and demonstrates actual unworkability; it does not create a separate watershed classification. KW and actual H/Q address particular defects and reliance. The freeze preserves those precise reasons while refusing automatic adoption of historical III-C. No claim is made that history itself lacked this additional reasoning. |

The departures above remain inferential modeling choices with material limits. There is no factual basis to say the historical Justices did not reconsider their earlier views: the historical opinion expressly says they did. The simulation’s different choice is the choice recorded **before** seeing that account and supported by each Justice’s specified earlier writings and actual public continuity. The record must disclose the difference, not describe it as unavoidable. The comparator does not itself supply an additional post-freeze position. No proposition receives another Justice’s signature by reason of historical joint authorship.

## Source corrections and completeness checks exposed by the comparator

1. The historical joint text’s emergency quotation ends with an OCR/miscitation to§3208; the definition is§3203, verified by the full statutory appendix and both lower courts. The long spousal quotation contains a reference to§3218(c); the neutral source identifies§3128(c). These are not new legal premises and require no neutral refreeze because the frozen packet already uses the validated provisions.
2. The historical introductory spousal summary omits some statutory detail and speaks loosely about consequences. Final descriptions must use the full statute: signed nonnotarized statements, punishable-falsity warning, diligent-effort location condition, the particular reported-assault route, reason to believe bodily injury likely to the woman by spouse/another, independent medical emergency, and actual penalty terms. The summary cannot convert possible professional suspension/revocation into inevitable revocation for every violation.
3. The historical opinion’s compressed information description does not display every permission. The full appendix confirms the rape information **may** be omitted; no police condition appears. The frozen packet already preserves it, and no commitment is affected.
4. The joint parental paragraph’s reference to informed consent “as defined above” and deliberation benefits does not justify fabricating an express statutory parent-specific waiting clock or personal-delivery mechanism. Full lower-court/source-text analysis remains necessary and was already performed.
5. Historical V-E’s broad research explanation and list of reports do not displace the distinction between confidential data collection and public release, the two different funding lookbacks, the actual limited referral-refusal findings, or the appeal’s scope. The source corrections are already in the neutral packet.
6. References within the historical opinion to post-divergence Riggins or Norman cannot enter this simulation as the historical versions of those cases. The freeze relies on the verified actual public current-term law; this review has added no historical post-divergence holding.

## Gate and remaining obligations

No unsupported factual discrepancy or missing date-eligible source emerged requiring neutral refresh. The principal doctrinal discrepancies are expressly supported by the full own writings and earlier public positions stated above, and remain disclosed inferential departures with the historical counterevidence acknowledged. The intrasection marital-status/severability choice is supported by the frozen statutory clause and record scope but is **not** a historical outcome confirmation; final assembly must preserve its distinct remedial status.

This reconciliation does not determine the final simulated opinion topology, whether a later proposed narrower formulation can gather more joins, or any assignment. In particular it supplies no warrant to publish a historical O’Connor–Kennedy–Souter joint opinion merely because it existed. Stone remains unopened. The final join audit must test each actual proposed proposition against the freeze, and a new general-framework commitment must receive a substantive reconciliation before it is counted.


# Casey historical-comparator reconciliation — Blackmun, Stevens, Souter

## Stage and source record

The root operator declared the global eight-Justice freeze complete and preserved the all-eight commitments and `neutral-packet-at-global-freeze.md` before authorizing this comparison. This agent then read **all** of the freshly retrieved CAP historical joint opinion (opinion0,134,197characters, including statutory appendix), the full historical Stevens opinion (opinion1,22,633characters, including all notes), and the full historical Blackmun opinion (opinion2,44,140characters, including all notes). Source: [505 U.S.833 CAP JSON](https://static.case.law/us/505/cases/0833-01.json), saved as `historical-casey.json`; extracted texts/source metadata are in the same scratch directory. The joint text was read in nine contiguous15,000character slices and Blackmun in three. Nothing was omitted because it was a footnote or appendix. The source shows the historical Court, not the simulated Court.

The controlling simulated commitments for this comparison are the final original cells plus the explicit pre-global-freeze scope/content addenda in `provisional-blackmun-stevens-souter.md`. No row is changed by this comparison. No Stone text or prior simulated Casey decision was opened. Historical authorship, historical collaboration and historical joinders are reported only to identify the historical propositions; none becomes a simulated assignment, opinion, or vote by default. Date-eligible source keys BR/BT/BW/BA/BO/ST/SW/SH/SO/SR/SP and public-source keys F/R/L/M/C/H/Q/Mc/Hu/W are defined precisely in that frozen matrix and `entering_law.md`.

**Classification:** “Confirms” means the previously frozen position agrees at the stated level. “Departure” means the simulated position differs in disposition, ground, scope or generality and the pre-freeze independent basis is identified. “Historical limit” means the comparator does not supply an independently separable position; none is invented from silence or a section-wide join/refusal. The original record/statute was not different in history. A newly examined fact or textual qualification may explain a different application from the earlier simulated brief, but it is **not** a claim that the historical Justices were applying a different statute.

## Exact historical baseline used

The historical joint writing announces a five-Justice opinion for the protected-choice, constitutional-liberty, stare-decisis, emergency, spousal-notice and concluding parts; Stevens and Blackmun join those parts. Its general substantial-obstacle framework, adult informed-consent discussion and informed-parent discussion speak for O'Connor, Kennedy and Souter. Its reporting discussion is joined by Stevens, for four. The historical judgment supporters from other opinions are not thereby joint-rationale joiners.

Historically, Blackmun retains strict scrutiny for more-than-de-minimis interference and Roe's trimester implementation. He accepts core choice, life/health protection and the emergency construction, agrees with notice invalidation under strict scrutiny, approves genuinely medical content including probable gestational age, rejects exclusive physician delivery and compulsory ancillary counseling, invalidates fixed delay and informed-parent burdens, and objects to the challenged referring/medical-judgment and public-report provisions. His own conclusion broadly seeks reversal in providers' docket91-744, but his expressed medical-content approval is not erased by that disposition formula.

Historically, Stevens retains core choice, life/health priority and stare decisis, rejects the joint writing's understanding of trimester limits and its persuasion rationale, and expressly proposes judging both severity and legitimate justification. He accepts the ordinary procedure/risk and childbirth-risk content, rejects the gestational-age and three ancillary commands as unnecessary, invalidates fixed delay, accepts a proper parental-consent/bypass architecture but refuses the informed-parent part because it depends on the adult counseling reasoning he rejects. He joins the joint reporting part. His refusal to join the entire adult-counseling part does not by itself prove a distinct recorded vote on every atom of its reasoning.

Historically, Souter is responsible for the entire joint writing, including replacement of rigid trimesters, an independently impermissible purpose/effect substantial obstacle, permission for reasonably related nonobstructive persuasion, factual insufficiency as the reason to uphold physician delivery/delay, and partial overruling of Akron/Thornburgh. The joint writing also recognizes physician speech interests while rejecting the particular clinical-disclosure objection as reasonable medical regulation. It does not separately decide the spousal First Amendment theory or standalone emergency vagueness.

The historical reporting treatment is broader than its exposition of individual fields: it upholds all challenged reports except the notice-linked item and explains medical research/confidentiality/cost generally. Its invalidation of §3214(a)(12) identifies the forced reasons for failing to notify, connected to notice's coercion. The frozen record's separation of confidential marital status from notice compliance is therefore more explicit than the historical account; it is not represented as a quotation or a precise historical subfield holding. The joint writing remands with severability for further proceedings. No historical opinion gives a new, independent holding on the unappealed underlying ordinary data items or every statutory exception.

## Adversarial reconciliation of the main departures

**Blackmun's three confidential medical-basis reports.** The strongest evidence against the frozen sustention is his historical explicit rejection of medical-judgment reporting coupled with the district chilling findings. That evidence is not disguised as confirmation. The frozen alternative rests on his own BA reasoning that written medical reasons can ensure reasoned choice, BT's permission for confidential health-directed reports, and BW's distinction between medically appropriate ascertainment and useless additional testing. Adequate confidentiality does not defeat the separate referral objection, and the freeze invalidates that identity item. The selected distinction is thus narrower than a general reporting preference. It is legally supported, not forced by the sources; history shows the other assessment was possible. No corrected source requires changing the frozen report conclusions.

**Blackmun's attendance disposition.** His historical opinion accepts the district's operational expectation as sufficient and rejects bypass as a cure. The freeze also rejects universal bypass cure, but treats source status as decisive for a facial injunction against the entire statute: finding232 expressly denies an enacted visit rule, fn23 reserves construction, and the statute permits independently operated ordinary informed consent. BO's focus on actual statutory barriers and BA's demand not to invent state text provide a Justice-specific basis. This is a narrower disposition, not denial that clinic practice can burden women. The existing ancillary-content partial reversal remains; the no-special-remand judgment was fixed before comparison.

**Stevens's gestational-age and ancillary sustention.** These are the most contested substantive differences. His historical application of ST/SH principles finds categorical information useless for many women and therefore undue. The freeze makes a different, already stated application: true gestational age informs clinical choices without requiring new tests; assistance/support facts can counter material financial misinformation; the materials notice informs a voluntary option rather than compelling reading or agreement. ST/SW preserve actual informed choice, SH accepts useful decisional structures while rejecting State preference alone, SO cautions against facial invalidation without the necessary implementation showing, and the earlier public C/R approach demands the actual constitutional burden and fit. BT is substantial contrary authority and was tested in each frozen row. It would be unsupported to say historic Stevens accepted these commands or that the current rape permission/optional receipt did not exist historically. It is also unsupported to convert the limited sustention into assent to the joint opinion's general State-persuasion rule. With those limitations, the departures have an identified pre-freeze basis; they are not uniquely determined predictions about the historical man.

**Stevens's referring-name invalidation.** History's reporting join is contrary evidence. The freeze relies on BT's concern with deterrence, BA's rejection of unsupported professional burdens and SH's scrutiny of actual fit: two doctors would stop even with sound confidentiality, while the performing physician can supply needed clinical information. The distinct data/enforcement rationale for confidential medical-basis reports remains intact. No assumption of leakage drives the difference. The model's evidence weight differs from history openly.

**Souter's delay, exclusive delivery and comprehensive-framework boundary.** Historical Souter unquestionably adopted the joint framework and sustained these two conditions. The freeze cannot call him undecided historically or use absence of earlier abortion writings as proof that he affirmatively rejected undue-burden doctrine. The independent simulated basis is SP's need for special justification, his exact public Q/H treatment of separable precedent, R's actual State-justification/alternatives holding, and M/W/C's caution about generalized transplantation. Under that method the freeze retains directly applicable adult-delay/delegation precedent, while distinguishing narrower truthful content and actual confidentiality. The unresolved question remains whether a fully specified new rule can provide the needed justification; the frozen bounded conclusion withholds endorsement of either comprehensive replacement formula until its actual propositions are reviewed. This comparison does not resolve that uncertainty by borrowing the historical outcome. Any subsequent movement must be recorded as a reconciliation of a circulated legal formulation, with precise affected rows and reasons, not as retrospective confirmation.

## Check result and unsupported-inference flags

No newly discovered historical fact establishes that a frozen row relied on a nonexistent statute, finding, source or date-eligible writing. No commitment was altered during comparison. The principal differences above are contested **substantive/interpretive departures**, not historical source corrections and not automatic consequences of Stone replacing Rehnquist.

The following inferences would be unsupported and are explicitly barred from assembly: that Stevens historically sustained ancillary/gestational commands; that Souter had a pre-Casey abortion writing rejecting the historical joint test; that Stevens's section-wide nonjoin uniquely settles an unreasoned physician-exclusivity subvote; that Blackmun's historical medical-report invalidation confirms the opposite frozen sustention; that the statutory optionality, rape permission or confidentiality were absent historically; that a judgment majority adopts the joint rationale; that historical shared authorship assigns any simulated opinion; or that equal results imply joinder in every ground. These flags are limitations on describing the evidence, not a source blocker requiring the comparator to supply a replacement vote.

**Handoff:** Stage3 reconciliation for these three Justices is complete. All original provisional commitments remain frozen. Await the root's confirmation that every other comparator reconciliation is complete before opening Stone's actual version1.2 and testing its precise propositions.


# Historical-comparator reconciliation — White, Scalia, Thomas

**Casey, Nos.91-744 and91-902, effective June29,1992. Stage3 only.** Root declared the all-eight commitments and neutral packet frozen before authorizing this comparison. The final White/Scalia/Thomas commitments are in the global frozen copy and `provisional-white-scalia-thomas.md`, including the spousal-speech/equality/vagueness refresh and final parental-content/attendance clarification. This file does not amend those commitments. No Stone supplement, prior simulated Casey record, or old Casey modeling has been opened.

## Source and full-read certification

Read **both full historical writings including their notes**, freshly extracted from `historical-casey.json`, Caselaw Access Project,505U.S.833: opinion3, Rehnquist,73,857characters; opinion4, Scalia,50,199characters. Six sequential14,000-character chunks cover the first; four cover the second, without omitted tails. Also read the exact runtime Casey comparator extract `runtime-comparator-casey.md`. The runtime extract's Justia link was not used for primary validation. Raw source is the CAP opinion, not an aggregator summary.

The historical Rehnquist writing states that White, Scalia and Thomas join it. The historical Scalia writing states that Rehnquist, White and Thomas join it. Those are **historical comparator facts only**. They do not assign an author, establish a joinder or place Rehnquist on the simulated Court. No proposed current writing has yet been circulated to this modeler. The historical joint opinion and other separate writings are assigned to other readers; this report does not claim a fresh full read of those by this modeler or independently validate their exact Court-wide counts.

Research cutoff for decisional authority: **June29,1992**. The comparator's same-day references to earlier historical decisions such as Lee or R.A.V. are not imported in place of the simulated public decisions. The frozen model's actual earlier-term positions remain its continuity baseline.

## Overall result of comparison

The comparator **confirms all three primary judgment directions**: each historical Justice would sustain the challenged provisions, including spousal notice, reject Roe's fundamental abortion-right treatment, abandon the trimester/viability restraint and decline the joint undue-burden approach. This confirmation supplies no original position: White's frozen path began with his own Roe/Doe, Danforth and Thornburgh writings; Scalia's with Webster, Ohio Akron, Riley and Michael H.; Thomas's with his actual simulated general-method writings and the stated uncertainty over McCollum/Quill adherence. Thomas's historical joinders confirm the selected bounded conclusion but do not retroactively create a preexisting abortion writing or eliminate that modeled uncertainty.

There are **important differences of expressed ground or scope**, principally the independently specified equality analysis, the express reservation concerning the distinct spousal-speech theory and preservation, item-by-item reporting grounds, exact statutory qualifications and refusal to pronounce a new standalone vagueness holding. The historical writings often announce overall sustention without separately addressing those questions. Silence is not proof that a historical Justice rejected or accepted the newly express rationale. Accordingly the table distinguishes confirmation, additional explicit ground, and an unresolved historical comparison rather than manufacturing a vote departure.

## Source keys used for the date-eligible independent basis

- **W-Roe:** White's Doe/Roe dissent,410U.S.221–223 (January22,1973), expressly reserving unpresented medical-necessity situations.
- **W-Danforth:** White's concurrence/dissent,428U.S.92–101 (July1,1976), including independent paternal interest, parental judgment, medical/evidentiary and severance reasoning.
- **W-Thornburgh:** White's dissent,476U.S.786–814 (June11,1986), constitutional reconsideration, accurate information, misleading-information limit, reporting and explicit anti-harassment reservation, medical judgment and bypass.
- **W-MichaelM:** Brennan's dissent joined by White,450U.S.488–496 (March23,1981), actual important objective/substantial relationship; **W-Caban:** Powell's Court opinion joined by White,441U.S.380 (April24,1979), same governing sex-classification test and rejection of universal parental-role assumptions.
- **Riley-W / S-Riley:** Riley,487U.S.781 (June29,1988), Court opinion joined by White and Scalia except the latter's refusal of footnote11, plus Scalia's own concurrence,803–804. Protected expression does not become commercial just because the speaker is compensated; compulsory factual advocacy is not automatically permissible; separate State record collection remains distinguishable.
- **S-Webster:**492U.S.532–537 (July3,1989); **S-Ohio:**497U.S.520–521 (June25,1990); **S-MichaelH:**491U.S.110 (June15,1989), Scalia's full plurality including his footnote6, exact entitlement/classification and preservation limits. These are actual own writings, not labels.
- **Public continuity:** Collins/Hudson in output chunk5 (February26/25,1992); Foucha/Riggins in chunk9 (May18); Quill in chunk9 (May26); McCollum/Wright in chunk12 (June18/19); Medina/Lee in chunk13 (June22/24); White's Hilton join in chunk3 (December16,1991). Thomas did not participate in Hilton. Every reference below is to that Justice's actual published writing or join in the named simulated entry, at the limits cataloged in the frozen matrix.

## Departures, additional grounds and unsupported inferences

### No unsupported primary judgment departure identified

All three selected primary provision votes match the historical Justices' actual disclosed positions. That is a confirmation after the freeze, not a reason for those positions. Their different earlier simulated public decisions were considered in the frozen rows, particularly White's actual bodily-liberty holdings, Scalia's strong Riley commitments, and Thomas's actual adherence to disputed precedent. No claim is made that the historical lineup compelled the modeled outcome.

### Express scope/ground differences requiring accurate recording

1. **Sex equality:** White's affirmative intermediate-scrutiny ground and Scalia/Thomas's assumption of that standard are distinct express propositions not found in these historical writings. They are supported as applications of date-eligible governing law and own method, but **cannot be described as historical confirmations of that precise rationale**. The frozen result remains defensible as an inference; the strongest counterargument remains coercive husband-only legal power despite notice's formal nonveto form. If a later circulated writing sharpens that into new male authority or erases actual abuse findings, none has a frozen join to it.
2. **Spousal speech reservation:** the present model expressly declines a newly independent merits ruling on an incompletely established preservation record, without finding waiver. The historical writings' whole-statute language is broader in presentation, while their exact First Amendment disposition is not separately knowable from these sources. Record the present reservation and its ordinary-remand consequences; do not call it a confirmed historical refusal to reach, or manufacture a contradictory historical merits ruling.
3. **Reporting details and conditional remedy:** the historical opinions group reports. Every frozen field-specific analysis remains necessary, including referral chill, three different medical-judgment predicates, public-release/collection separation, two funding lookbacks and the no-funding evidentiary condition. A conditional severance concurrence on notice-linked reporting remains **unformed/unjoined** until assembly; there is no historical basis for counting it automatically.
4. **Emergency:** the historical Rehnquist opinion ends with broad 'significant threat' language. The frozen model rests on the actual prospective statutory and Third Circuit construction, which the neutral record distinguishes from negligible risk and significant risk of only transient harm. Do not import a new universal all-health exception by detached quotation of the comparator. Likewise no standalone vagueness holding is supplied by overall sustention.
5. **Facial review:** historical Rehnquist explicitly invokes Salerno's no-valid-application rule. The frozen models reject the proposed abortion denominator and sustain facially at their specified sources, but do not all adopt an independently sufficient universal Salerno rule in their own words. Adding that proposition now requires a join/reconciliation test; historical join alone is not a stage3 license to broaden the freeze.
6. **Speech method:** historical silence does not authorize a finding that all paid medical speech is commercial, all truthful compelled facts are permitted, all optional information cures compulsion, or that rejecting Roe disposes of speech. Riley-W/S-Riley and Thomas's source-specific method remain operative barriers.

### Exact statutory qualifications remain source-controlled

The frozen packet's statutory appendix, not either opinion's compressed summary, controls every exception. In particular, paternal-support information in rape **may** be omitted; there is no reporting condition and no obligation to omit. Spousal notice substitutes require a signed nonnotarized statement with false-statement warning that spouse is not father, diligent effort cannot locate him, §3128 spousal assault was reported to law enforcement with jurisdiction, or the woman has reason to believe notice likely will cause bodily injury to her by spouse or another. District finding297 identifies the incorporated assault law's90-day reporting restriction. The medical emergency is the physician's good-faith clinical judgment of immediate abortion to avert death or delay creating serious risk of substantial and irreversible impairment of major bodily function. No general psychological/economic/child-injury/divorce/custody/publicity exception is added. The disclosure-health defense requires preponderance proof of reasonable belief that furnishing information would severely adversely affect physical or mental health; it is not a delay waiver. Public-funding timing is12calendar months before an inspection/copy request under§3207(b), but before filing under§3214(f), with the latter's no-funding representation confidential unless contrary evidence establishes funding. Parental statutory text and its alternate consents/bypass must be quoted in the final entry, without inventing a parent-specific waiting or delivery mechanism.

## Reconciliation determination and handoff

**No frozen primary commitment is changed by this comparator review.** No authorship, assignment, opinion join or conditional remedial vote has been adopted. The comparator confirms broad framework/provision positions, while newly explicit independent grounds and reach reservations remain supported by the date-eligible own-writing/public/source reasons recorded before it was opened. No unsupported primary departure was found; the limitations above are mandatory qualifications on any claim of historical confirmation.

The modeler remains unexposed to Stone and to the prior simulated Casey record. For a later §8 audit, send only the relevant frozen commitment, sanitized proposed formulation, actual neutral facts, proposed join/refusal and asserted reason for movement. A matching judgment or historical co-signature is not sufficient. No new material factual premise was discovered in these two comparator readings that requires changing the neutral packet; historical assertions and paraphrases were not substituted for the already validated trial/statutory sources.
