### Preserved audit material — compatibility oconnor kennedy

# O’Connor/Kennedy compatibility with Stone v1.2

**Stage4/section8 compatibility only.** After the global neutral/commitment freeze and completed historical reconciliation, this reviewer read **all150lines of the complete user v1.2 source**, `terms/OT1991/briefs/OT_1991CHUNK14_CASEY_V1_2.md`, including its source corrections, six steps, applications and controlled disposition. No tail was omitted. This document tests the approved position as written; it does not rewrite Stone, assign an opinion, import historical authorship or change a reconciled commitment.

Authority keys have the precise meanings and full-read certifications in `provisional-oconnor-kennedy.md` and `reconciliation-oconnor-kennedy.md`. In particular: **OA/OT/OW/OH/OHS/OG/OM** are O’Connor’s Akron/Thornburgh dissents, Webster/Hodgson concurrences, actual joined Hodgson parts, Hogan opinion and Michael H. concurrence. **KW/KH/KO/KP/KJ/KM** are Kennedy’s Webster joinder, Hodgson/Ohio writings, Payne concurrence joinder, Texas v.Johnson concurrence and Michael H. joinder. **R,L,M,C,Q/H,X** identify the exact simulated Riggins/Foucha, Lee, Medina, Collins, Quill/Hilton, Hudson/Wright public positions. These are date-eligible sources; historical Casey supplied only the separately recorded reconciliation.

**Reading of results:** “Join” is a direct compatibility conclusion for the identified isolated proposition, not a signature on Stone’s full framework. “Partial” identifies exactly what is compatible and what prevents the full passage. “Refuse” preserves a frozen disagreement. A minimum revision describes a possible separate common formulation; it is **not** authority to alter Stone’s approved text or make him adopt a premise he rejects. Every provision judgment and remedy must still receive its own count.

## Six steps and proof/review

| Stone proposition | O’Connor: compatibility, objection, minimum revision | Kennedy: compatibility, objection, minimum revision |
|---|---|---|
| Step1: identify precise obligation, stage, construction, exceptions, enforcement and added conduct | **Join as an analytic instruction.** OA/OT/OW demand actual regulation and record rather than abstractions. Identifying conduct beyond ordinary care is not by itself a presumption that it is unconstitutional. No revision needed if this remains identification rather than Step5’s burden shift. | **Join.** KW/KO examine actual statutory language and a workable construction. No revision needed; no general invalidity follows from the identification alone. |
| Step1: previability decision needs no official approval of reasons or showing medical necessity; care remains clinically appropriate; demonstrably unsafe practice unprotected | **Join at the retained-choice level.** OW/OH/OM and the frozen Roe boundary support it. The distinction between deciding to end pregnancy and the safety of the method is material. No revision needed; other regulations remain separately reviewable. | **Join.** KW recognizes protected liberty while permitting medical regulation; KM prevents reducing liberty to the narrowest historical formulation. No automatic joinder to a new general obstacle test. |
| Step1: after viability, prohibition subject to necessary life/health protection | **Join the retained Roe boundary**, preserving OT’s reserved calibration of particular health/fetal-survival tradeoffs. No newly decided method or risk dispute may be attributed from this general sentence. | **Join the retained boundary**, with KW’s useful/prudent medical-regulation limits. No new decision on every postviability prohibition follows. |
| Step2: identify actual purpose/design/operation; legitimate safety, informed-choice, parental and useful accountability interests; opposition alone is not unlawful | **Join those propositions.** OA/OT/OH and OW funding joins accept those interests; the actual inquiry remains required. | **Join.** KW/KH/KO support distinct legitimate interests; KJ prevents public commitment alone from overriding an established constitutional command. |
| Step2: independently invalidate a purpose of obstructing choice, proved objectively | **Partial.** She can join invalidation of a measure whose objective is to prevent the protected decision or impose third-party control, and a measure lacking any reasonable legitimate purpose. OA/OT do not support treating any intentionally added regulatory friction as independently fatal despite a reasonable legitimate function. Minimum: define obstruction as defeating protected choice, distinguish reasonable deliberation/medical responsibility, and do not infer illicit purpose merely from intended persuasion or additional process. | **Partial for the same narrow anti-prevention proposition.** KW/KH permit deliberate regulatory structure serving legitimate purposes. His freeze did not select a universal independent-purpose rule for every mixture of legitimate and obstructive motives. Minimum: objective purpose to defeat the protected decision, not merely a rule intended to influence it through reasonable information/process. Broader adoption needs section8 audit. |
| Step3: noncircular affected group, genuinely irrelevant persons excluded, State causation distinguished from poverty/geography/abuse/provider scarcity themselves | **Join the analytic propositions.** OHS/L examine actual operation; C requires State attribution. This does not automatically adopt Step6’s general facial large-fraction rule. | **Join the analytic propositions.** KH distinguishes actual regulated setting, L actual pressure, C State causation. KO’s ordinary facial rule is not silently superseded. |
| Step4: actual visits/delay/cost/privacy/clinical/capacity effects and interactions, no double counting or unsupported assumption | **Join as evidence rules**, OA/OT’s actual severe-burden inquiry and OHS’s detailed family record. Cumulative proof can matter without adopting meaningful-burden balancing. | **Join as evidence rules**, KW/KH/KO and L; interactions cannot be ignored merely because separately described. This is not adoption of a new comprehensive threshold. |
| Step4: alternatives count only if realistically usable; reliable predictions may suffice without actual denied care | **Join with its stated reliable-evidence limitation.** OHS recognizes operational obstacles; prospective challenge need not await injury. OH nevertheless says an adequate bypass can cure the parental burden despite its inconvenience. Minimum clarification if needed: usable does not mean costless or perfect in every case. | **Join with reliable proof and practical usability.** KH/KO require genuine safeguards but reject hypothetical worst-case administration and a demand for perfect results. The text does not itself require that perfection; do not add it in application. |
| Step5: meaningful burden triggers State substantiation of benefit/fit | **Refuse.** OA/OT/OH select severity before exacting scrutiny; below severity rational review applies. Riggins’s justification burden is tied to involuntary medication and M expressly rejects universal transfer. Minimum material revision: severe limitation triggers the stronger State justification inquiry; smaller burdens need a reasonable relation, without automatic evidentiary burden shift. This changes Stone’s actual proposal and cannot be described as a shared framework. | **Refuse.** KW/KH/KO permit reasonable nonpreclusive regulation without requiring State proof for each nontrivial burden; R/Foucha/M keep constitutional channels distinct. Minimum: reasonable relationship and the independently protected choice; a new general burden scale needs audited reconsideration. |
| Step5: substantial obstacle to protected, medically appropriate care independently invalid | **Partial, not a full join.** Stone’s express allowance for necessary action against demonstrated medical danger is material and prevents misdescribing him as protecting unsafe practice. Nevertheless OA/OT reserve possible compelling justification after severe burden, including the potential-life argument in her prior delay analysis; she froze only the categorical anti-veto/protected-decision limit and unjustified severe burdens. Minimum: state that independently prohibited third-party control is invalid and otherwise retain the compelling-justification stage. The word “protected” cannot by itself settle the disputed general rule. | **Partial.** The particular effective third-party veto is forbidden, and genuine safety rules cannot redefine the right away by label. His freeze does not adopt a universal automatic rule for every serious access restriction. Stone’s danger qualification is acknowledged but does not erase the remaining difference. Minimum: specific anti-veto rule plus separately tested necessary health regulation; broader general formulation requires section8 audit. |
| Step5: smaller burden invalid for inadequate justification, excessive burden/benefit ratio or unexplained practical less burdensome alternative | **Refuse full rule.** OA/OT reject minimum-cost constitutional administration below severity. Concrete alternatives can expose irrationality or inform exacting review once triggered, but do not automatically shift proof for every meaningful burden. Minimum: restrict enhanced fit/alternatives scrutiny to the appropriate established trigger. | **Refuse.** KW/KH reject perfect fit and tolerate useful cost. Alternatives are relevant facts, not a universally required governmental defense. Minimum: reasonable fit for this nonpreclusive regulation; no comprehensive necessity formula. |
| Step5: necessary safety measures remain possible; State must establish function and rejected alternatives whenever access materially reduced | **Partial.** Join permission for genuine safety regulation, rejection of a bare safety label, and no demand for perfect policy/scientific certainty/new study. Refuse automatic heightened proof from every material reduction. OA/OT/OW require the proper severity trigger. Minimum: maintain substantive clinical protection and select proof according to the adopted standard. | **Partial.** KW supports useful/non-dangerous medical regulation and evidence-based construction, but not a State alternatives burden whenever access materially changes. Minimum: same narrow substantive limits, no automatic threshold migration. |
| Step6: exact invalid duty/dependencies, lawful severability, unaffected people do not defeat an individual claim | **Join.** OW/OT’s scope discipline, OHS individual protection and Q/X proposition separation. No revision needed; the identification of which obligations are invalid remains contested. | **Join.** KW’s unappealed-scope discipline, KH individual protected decision and H/Q actual separability. No revision needed. |
| Step6: general qualification of Ohio’s no-valid-applications approach with large-fraction facial rule | **Refuse general adoption; join the narrower notice application.** Her KO joins and OW’s facial discipline remain frozen. OHS/L support the actually restricted spousal group; that does not establish a general formula for all obligations tested under Step5. Minimum: describe this notice condition’s demonstrated operation and preserve ordinary facial rules elsewhere. | **Refuse general replacement of KO; join this record’s narrow notice relief.** KO is his own specific facial analysis. KH’s minor/adult and safeguard distinctions support the notice result without adopting a universal large-fraction calculus. Minimum: same case-specific statement. |
| Proof/review: challenger proves obligation/group/causation and meaningful burden or illicit purpose; State then proves fit | **Refuse threshold/allocation package.** Same OA/OT/OH barrier as Step5. Ordinary factual-review rules do not cure the substantive burden-shift disagreement. Minimum: her actual severity/justification sequence, with separately sourced prohibited-purpose rules. | **Refuse package.** Same KW/KH/KO/M barrier; no general adopted formula authorizes the shift. Minimum: ordinary proof with actual specific constitutional predicates. |
| Proof/review: ordinary factual rules, separate findings, independent constitutional review; no universal heightened quantum; no alteration of §3205(c) | **Join these severable procedural limits.** OT insists on accurate record/posture and M distinguishes context-specific proof. No independent vagueness ruling is thereby adopted. | **Join these limits**, KW/KO/M/X. No independent vagueness holding or universal new review standard for adjudicative facts is implied. |

## Constitutional, medical and stare-decisis propositions

| Stone proposition | O’Connor | Kennedy |
|---|---|---|
| National constitutional floor; States can protect more and reasonably regulate qualifications, consent, safety and appropriate reporting | **Join**, OA/OT/OW/OH. Does not imply every extra State burden requires Step5 substantiation. | **Join**, KW/KH/KO; protected choice is real, regulation remains possible. |
| Potential life legitimate; lawful childbirth support/persuasion; moral disapproval not independent justification for obstructing the protected decision; fewer abortions through impediments cannot justify those impediments themselves | **Join narrow anti-circularity/protected-choice proposition.** OA/OT allow informed deliberation to persuade; their argument cannot be transformed into a vote that every added cost meant to encourage care is unlawful. Minimum if incorporated broadly: define the independently impermissible obstruction and preserve reasonable persuasion. | **Join same narrow proposition**, KW/KH/KJ. Reasoned preference is legitimate, but defeating the retained decision is not justified merely by calling its defeat beneficial. No universal rule excluding moral considerations from legislation. |

## Precisely worded common propositions available without movement

These are proposed standalone public legal propositions for testing with the other Justices. Neither “majority” nor “Court” status is asserted before their direct joins are counted. Both O’Connor and Kennedy can join each text below for their already frozen reasons. Stone compatibility remains for root to test against his actual stated limits.

### Protected choice and safety

“The Constitution protects the woman’s decision to terminate a pregnancy before viability. The State may pursue maternal health and potential life throughout pregnancy, but it may not prohibit that protected decision or place its exercise under another person’s control. The patient need not establish an officially approved reason for ending a previability pregnancy; the care provided remains subject to legitimate safety regulation. After viability the State may restrict abortion subject to the retained protection for abortion necessary, in appropriate medical judgment, to preserve the woman’s life or health.”

This text supplies the shared core, not a universal threshold for every regulation. O’Connor does not surrender the possible compelling-justification stage for other severe burdens; Kennedy does not adopt a complete scale. The frozen health-risk reservations remain. Sources: OW/OH/OM; KW/KH/KM/KP.

### Isolated negative trimester proposition

“Roe’s rigid trimester timetable no longer supplies categorical immunity from reasonable regulation merely because care occurs in the first trimester.”

**Both directly join this exact sentence.** OA/OT and KW provide direct reasons; no movement or new source is required. Its signers must be counted separately from the signers of protected-choice retention. A Justice who rejects Roe’s constitutional foundation may agree with this negative proposition without joining that foundation.

### Ordinary stare decisis

“Reconsideration must identify the particular proposition whose workability, legal foundation or factual premises are challenged. The demonstrated defects in Roe’s rigid regulatory timetable do not establish a defect in its separate protection of the previability decision. The settled ability to plan reproductive life and obtain lawful care supports retaining that protection. Revising the timetable therefore does not withdraw the constitutional choice. The particular rules governing the challenged obligations, and the remedy for each, must be decided separately.”

Both directly join this bounded reason. OA/OT/OW and Q/H justify O’Connor’s position; KW/KP and H/Q justify Kennedy’s. It neither adds historical Casey’s special watershed-legitimacy doctrine nor conceals their differing treatment of specific Akron/Thornburgh applications. A separate sentence identifying exactly which precedent rules are displaced must be tested against the actual resulting judgments.

### Spousal notice

“The State may encourage voluntary consultation within marriage, but it may not condition protected abortion care on disclosure that, in the relationships established by this record, gives a spouse practical control of the woman’s decision. The constitutional inquiry addresses the women whom the legal notice condition actually compels to disclose; it is not diluted by women who would notify voluntarily. The record establishes that the condition enables prevention and serious coercion in that operative group. The statutory alternatives do not adequately remove that operation. The notice condition and its dependent notice/nonnotice-reason reporting cannot stand.”

For publication the same entry must state the actual statutory terms: signed nonnotarized notice or substitute statement, warning of punishable falsity, spouse not father, inability to locate after diligent effort, §3128 assault reported to competent law enforcement with district297’s incorporated90-day restriction, reason to believe notice likely causes bodily injury to the woman by spouse or another, and independent medical emergency. The reasons economic/psychological/child-directed coercion remain unaddressed must be explained. Neither Justice needs a universal large-fraction rule or general automatic-obstacle test to join this narrower sufficient ground. O’Connor’s additional equality/privacy grounds may appear separately; Kennedy does not acquire them by joining this text.

### Narrow delivery and waiting permission

“The record establishes added expense and delay but does not establish severe restriction of access. Assigning these medical disclosures to a physician reasonably serves medical responsibility, and the fixed interval reasonably serves informed deliberation. Those conclusions sustain these requirements on this record; they do not announce a comprehensive test governing other abortion regulations.”

**Both can directly join this limited paragraph.** O’Connor’s actual severity threshold is addressed before reasonable justification; Kennedy accepts the case-specific finding without adopting her full framework. “Preclusive” should not substitute for “severe” if it suggests only total impossibility counts: OA/OT recognize severe limitations short of complete denial. The accepted burdens must be described accurately and the prospective emergency construction included in the final holding. Other Justices may share the narrow permission without joining protected-choice retention; direct proposition counts must show that distinction. Stone disagrees with these dispositions, so this paragraph cannot be labeled a common ground with him.

## General replacement standard and section8 movement gate

**No comprehensive replacement standard currently attracts both these Justices and Stone on the frozen commitments.** Both can join exact constitutional boundaries, the negative trimester proposition, the analytic evidence/ordinary review steps and several particular permissions/prohibitions. O’Connor alone retains her established severity/exacting-justification framework. Kennedy’s frozen rules are more contextual. Neither accepts Stone’s meaningful-burden State-proof/fit structure. Agreement that regulations must be “reasonable,” “not undue” or preserve “choice” cannot conceal those differences. A component majority can decide a particular requirement without promulgating a universal test, and a negative rejection of trimester machinery is itself distinct from adoption of any replacement.

No movement is recommended merely to produce a comprehensive majority. If root identifies an actual circulated formulation that resolves a recorded objection, section8 requires a **separate sanitized reconciliation audit receiving no Stone supplement/private material**. Its input must contain: frozen neutral packet, comparator, prior reconciled commitment, the exact proposed **public legal ground**, the objection it resolves and resulting scope/remedy change. The audit must identify each Justice’s own date-eligible basis and any new departure from history. An accepted narrower proposition already expressly within this compatibility matrix does not turn into acceptance of the rejected remainder. If a material new neutral fact/authority/premise is discovered instead, Engine§7 requires neutral refresh/remodel/reconciliation rather than a post-Stone patch.

This file records final compatibility, not fictional draft exchanges or a promise that either Justice changed views. Assignment and final coalition topology remain root’s section8 tasks.

# Blackmun, Stevens and Souter — actual Stone v1.2 compatibility

## Stage, object and limits

This is the post-Stone section 8 join test. The final provisional matrix and comparator reconciliation were frozen before Stone's text was opened. The modeler has now read the entire user-approved `terms/OT1991/briefs/OT_1991CHUNK14_CASEY_V1_2.md`, including the controlled dispositions, interim injunctions and before-rendering checklist. References to changes from v1.1 describe drafting history only. No earlier simulated Casey record supplies a position. Single-conversation mode used scoped handoffs; it did not provide true blindness or a fresh independent context.

The objects tested below are Stone's actual propositions, not the label undue burden. **J** means direct compatibility with the proposition at the stated scope. **P** means only the expressly identified severed proposition can be joined; the whole proposition cannot be counted. **R** means refusal of the actual proposition. A shared disposition never entails adoption of Stone's complete framework. Minimum revisions describe another possible published formulation; they do not authorize editing Stone's input. No assignment or invented circulation history appears here. No reconciled judgment vote changes in this report.

Source keys and exact full-read documentation are in the final frozen `provisional-blackmun-stevens-souter.md` and `reconciliation-blackmun-stevens-souter.md`. Principal own writings: BR = Blackmun, Roe (1973); BT = Blackmun, Thornburgh (1986), joined Stevens; BW = Blackmun, Webster (1989); BA = Blackmun, Ashcroft (1983), joined Stevens; BO = Blackmun, Ohio v. Akron Center (1990); ST = Stevens, Thornburgh (1986); SW = Stevens, Webster (1989); SH = Stevens, Hodgson (1990); SO = Stevens, Ohio v. Akron Center (1990); SR = Stevens's actual Riley joins (1988); SP = Souter, Payne concurrence (June 27, 1991), joined Kennedy. Souter has no date-eligible abortion writing. Earlier simulated public sources are F = Foucha and R = Riggins (May 18, 1992); Q = Quill (May 26); M = Medina (June 22); L = Lee (June 24); C = Collins (February 26); H = Hilton (December 16, 1991); W = Wright (June 19). Each use is limited to that Justice's actual public part/joinder, as cataloged in the frozen matrix. The research cutoff remains June 29, 1992.

Clinical appropriateness cannot become a requirement that the woman justify her previability decision medically. No revision required at this scope. |

Stevens requires retained meaningful-burden justification and fit as operative protection, not merely a congenial minority proposal. No independent extra authority merely because a controversy is intense or the Court's standing is implicated. No revision required with those limits. |

A shared opinion must sever ordinary clinical information and voluntary publication from mandatory ancillary commands. All three retain protection against coercion, distortion and medical interference. |

### Isolated negative timetable proposition

The root's proposed operative negative is: **“Roe's trimester timetable no longer determines when health and potential-life regulation may begin; regulation is not invalid solely because it occurs in the first trimester; the State's interests exist throughout pregnancy, subject to independently applicable constitutional limitations.”**

* **Blackmun refuses the operative abolition.** Roe never supplied immunity from every reasonable first-trimester regulation; BA expressly recognizes regulation without a significant impact that serves important health objectives. But that existing qualification is not the same proposition as eliminating the staged requirements. BW affirmatively preserves them. He can join the accurate statement that interests exist and that first-trimester timing alone does not invalidate an otherwise constitutionally permissible rule. He cannot be counted for removal of the restrictions governing what makes a rule permissible. Minimum revision: describe the existing nonimmunity and retained protections without “no longer” displacing the staged rules.
* **Stevens refuses bare operative erasure when his substantive condition is absent.** His willingness to replace mechanical stages rests on maintaining meaningful justification and fit for actual burdens. Stone's Steps 1–5 furnish a formulation Stevens can support in his own proposed rule. If that rule does not obtain a Court majority, availability of the minority proposal does not satisfy his condition for a separate operative vote that deletes the prior safeguards. ST, SW and SH require attention to actual burden and legitimate justification. Do not extract a seventh vote from half of that conditional position. Minimum revision: adopt the protected meaningful-burden rule as operative law, or limit the change to precise regulations the Court independently sustains while preserving other controlling protections. This is not a proposed alteration of Stevens's frozen judgment votes.
* **Souter refuses abolition by ambiguous “reasonableness.”** SP requires identifying the defect and special justification for the particular precedent displaced. His frozen commitment preserves direct regulation precedents unless that showing is made; it did not commit him to a complete new timetable or universal substitute. He can join the existing proposition that otherwise constitutionally permissible regulation does not fail solely from its first-trimester timing. Here “permissible” must incorporate applicable constitutional protection, not rationality alone. Minimum revision: state that narrower proposition or identify specific rules displaced with their own justification. Do not count him for general abolition.

Thus none of these three supplies an extra direct vote for the bare operative negative. Any six-Justice negative coalition consisting of Stone, White, O'Connor, Scalia, Kennedy and Thomas must rest on their actual joins; the three Justices denying protected choice cannot be counted for the separately retained protected-choice clause.

### Souter's comprehensive-rule reservation after actual text

The actual proposal resolves several possible drafting objections: it identifies an independent population rather than a circular denominator; requires causal, nontrivial burdens; admits competent contrary medical evidence; rejects a professional veto, scientific certainty and policy perfection; distinguishes an independently impermissible obstacle from a lesser burden; and separates constitutional review from ordinary factual review. Those are meaningful reasons Souter can join discrete methods and applications. They do **not** answer the remaining SP question: why a new universal balancing, alternatives and burden-transfer formulation should replace each retained abortion rule, or why the general large-fraction formula follows from his own prior method. His frozen position expressly left both a threshold-only and a comprehensive two-tier replacement unresolved. The bounded conclusion remains retention of the core, obstacle prohibition as a floor, concrete precedent-based applications, and no full framework joinder. This is uncertainty stated as an unresolved general question with determinate component votes, not back-filling from the historical joint opinion.

## Feasible common propositions and actual law inventory

Within these three, all directly join protected choice, viability, necessary life/health protection, the actual narrow stare-decisis explanation, protective emergency construction, ordinary clinical information, genuine voluntary consent, no unexplained hindsight punishment of reasonable clinical judgment, confidential useful medical reports under their actual limits, severance of notice-dependent fields with preservation of marital-status data, and spousal-notice invalidation on the practical-control/abortion-liberty rationale. They also agree that absence of a severe obstacle is not an automatic constitutional safe harbor for every lesser burden. That negative does not amount to a shared complete positive regulatory test.

A possible six-Justice protected-choice, stare-decisis or spousal coalition with Stone, O'Connor and Kennedy requires their own actual join reports; this report supplies B/Stevens/Souter only. An O'Connor independent equality/privacy rationale is not joined by these three as an independently sufficient holding. No complete Stone framework or historical joint formulation acquires their votes merely because the label or judgment matches.

All three may acknowledge an accurate account of what separately joined opinions establish while dissenting from particular resulting law. Such acknowledgment does not make them direct adherents of rejected timetable changes, adult-delay permissions, exclusive-delivery permissions, or another Justice's complete framework. The dedicated governing-standard holding should identify itself as an **authority statement assembled from the specified opinion parts and their distinct coalitions**, if that is its actual form. It must not be represented as an opinion joined by all six core-retention Justices on every operative sentence. A judgment majority is not an opinion majority. A Marks claim must identify both actual rationales and demonstrate the logical-subset relation; overlapping protected-choice conclusions alone do not make incompatible permission/burden rules nested.

## Root extended drafts reviewed

Both entries in `extended-drafts-root.json` were read completely, including the rules, explanations and precedent-treatment lines. Blackmun, Stevens and Souter can join **The framework retaining protected choice** and **Stare decisis and the constitutional foundation** as drafted, with the following scope made explicit in topology rather than erased by a global heading:

1. The statement that State interests exist throughout pregnancy is compatible with Blackmun's distinction between existence and the stronger justification required to burden care. It is not his joinder in the separate operative timetable abolition. The draft expressly says existence does not resolve a particular restriction's validity.
2. The explanation properly distinguishes protected decisionmaking from a medical-necessity justification for previability abortion and from immunity for unsafe practice. It does not use the case to establish drug admission, public funding, fetal personhood or unpresented postviability law.
3. Hilton and Quill are illustrations of precise, setting-specific precedent treatment. The draft expressly confines their statutory and commercial contexts and does not invent a unanimous Quill Commerce rationale, transplant congressional correction into constitutional abortion law or attribute Stone's minority position to these Justices. Their actual public joins support the analogy at that level.
4. The stare-decisis explanation rejects mere renewed disagreement and Court membership change as sufficient, addresses workability/foundation/reliance, and permits a specifically justified separable correction. That follows BW/ST/SP and the actual Q/H continuity without requiring Blackmun to agree that any particular staged rule should now be changed.
5. The draft adopts no independent superprecedent rule based on public controversy or institutional standing. All three can join the specified reasons without importing an additional historical legitimacy doctrine.
6. References to distinct coalitions and the dedicated governing-standard holding accurately acknowledge a fracture. They cannot be used to transform these two joined holdings into assent to every other part's substantive outcome.

## Reconciliation and final handoff

**Direct check of revised common materials/attendance formulations:** The four entries `lawful-publication-elective-receipt`, `particular-materials-focused-remedy`, `ordinary-informed-parent-content` and `parental-attendance` in `holdings-health-parent-spouse.json` were read in full after source-agent revision. All three directly join the first two: lawful publication/elective receipt is separate from compulsory notices, and denial of Stone's additional focused remedy neither certifies every publication nor immunizes actual misleading execution. Stevens and Souter join the ordinary informed-parent content entry; Blackmun's assent stops at ordinary medical content and excludes mandatory incorporation of the three ancillary notices. All three join the revised attendance rule and explanation, which accept the positive findings, leave the state-law construction open, identify no demonstrated unavailability or malfunction of the independent route, and expressly reject a universal claim that bypass cures every unnecessary visit. The Hodgson treatment must retain that limit: adequate independent authorization is a safeguard in the distinct parental-involvement setting, not a universal cure for needless attendance. These are actual narrow direct joins consistent with the frozen refusals of Stone's added vacaturs/interim orders; no judgment or ground moves.

**Actual speech-scope clarification:** Root's component review identified that the first compatibility version grouped clinical speech together with reserved independent grounds too broadly. The final frozen matrix expressly sustains ordinary medical content against the speech objection (Blackmun's speech row), rejects an automatic commercial shortcut while sustaining narrow truthful disclosures (Stevens's row), and upholds those disclosures while reserving a larger professional-speech standard (Souter's row). The distinct rows above restore that precise scope. All three can join a narrow clinical-disclosure First Amendment permission; Stevens and Souter also join the actual ancillary permission; Blackmun reserves the ancillary alternative ground after due-process invalidation. No frozen vote, ground or general doctrinal ceiling moves. The spousal-speech, equality/general-privacy and vagueness reservations remain unchanged.

**No actual non-Stone commitment moved.** Specificity in Stone's proposal permits discrete joins already within the frozen positions; unresolved comprehensive-formula reservations remain unresolved at their stated scope. Possible future movements — adoption of a generalized large-fraction test, Souter's universal two-tier/proof rule, or any of these three adopting Stone's attendance/materials vacaturs and interim injunctions — require an explicit sanitized section 8 reconciliation audit before commitment. Such an audit must identify the earlier position, actual newly circulated proposition or newly validated material, Justice-specific dated source, strongest objection and concrete reason for change. Desire for a majority, historic authorship and a shared outcome are not reasons.

The final votes and join ceilings in this document should be carried into every part-level join matrix. No opinion author is assigned here; assignment follows the actual judgment/proposition coalition and seniority under Engine section 8.

### Preserved audit material — sanitized joinder audit white scalia thomas

# Sanitized §8 compatibility audit — White, Scalia, Thomas

**Scope and exposure:** Root supplied only proposed public formulations and the relevant joining questions after the global freeze/comparator reconciliation. This auditor has not opened Stone's Casey supplement, the prior simulated Casey record or any private proposed complete opinion. The determinations below concern those exact formulations and final frozen commitments. They do not select the Court-wide result, invent circulation history, assign a Chief who has not been supplied as part of a coalition, or presume a historical author/joinder.

**Audit standard:** A Justice may directly join an independently sufficient, limited permission that rests on grounds the Justice accepts, even though another joiner would retain a constitutional foundation the Justice rejects. An isolated part must actually state that common permission; a matching disposition, common label or silent assumption cannot supply a join. The rejected converse—what would make another law unconstitutional—is not adopted by agreeing that this law is valid for the stated reasons. The frozen row's qualifications travel with the join.

## Proposed physician-delivery and waiting-period part

Root's latest exact formulation is:

> The record establishes added expense and delay but does not establish severe restriction of access. Assigning these medical disclosures to a physician reasonably serves medical responsibility, and the fixed interval reasonably serves informed deliberation. Those conclusions sustain these requirements on this record; they do not announce a comprehensive test governing other abortion regulations.

This replaces the earlier 'preclusive access' wording. **'Severe restriction' is not limited to absolute impossibility**, and this sentence does not assert that no individual ever experiences prevention or that added costs/delays are trivial. Its scope is the two facially challenged requirements on the established record. 'These medical disclosures' must refer to the performing/referring physician's accurate §3205(a)(1) material procedure/risks/alternatives, probable gestational age and childbirth-risk information. The fixed interval is the actual24-hour statutory timing, not a new parent-specific interval. The part must carry §3203's prospective emergency exemption: the physician's good-faith clinical judgment necessitates immediate abortion to avert death or delay creates serious risk of substantial and irreversible impairment of major bodily function. An explanation under§3205(b) is given before the abortion only if possible. The separate disclosure-health defense is not a waiting exemption.

| Justice | Direct joinder determination and accepted ground | Strongest objection and its resolution | Minimum revision / reconciliation status |
|---|---|---|---|

W-Thornburgh and W-Danforth already support reasonable professional responsibility and informed deliberation; his frozen rows accept competent counselors and substantial actual extra costs while finding the selected requirements valid. The sentence states a sufficient permission he independently accepts. It does not make his reason depend on a protected abortion right. | The record contains serious, disproportionate burdens and the sentence must not invent a no-burden finding or imply that physician delivery is necessary because counselors are incompetent. White can accept the limited absence-of-proved-severe-restriction conclusion alongside his rational-fit ground, without agreeing that severe restriction would invariably defeat that ground. | **No change needed to the quoted formulation** if the surrounding part gives the defined requirements, actual burdens and prospective emergency terms. If 'severe restriction' is glossed as absolute impossibility/no woman prevented, remove that gloss. If a surrounding sentence makes a threshold universally necessary or recognizes protected choice, White refuses that sentence. No substantive commitment movement. Sources: W-Thornburgh; W-Danforth; simulated Medina, Riggins and Wright; frozen delivery/delay/framework rows. |

This part addresses specified accurate medical disclosures and timing as a sufficient permission; it states neither forbidden proposition. The accepted counselor/cost findings remain intact. | **No change needed to the quoted formulation** on that scope. **Authority consequence:** If O'Connor and Kennedy independently join this same isolated part at this actual level, these three direct joins permit a **five-Justice opinion for the stated particular permissions and reasons**. That is not a five-Justice opinion adopting either their retained-right framework or a general rational-basis replacement for all abortion regulation. Neither the necessary conditions for invalidity in future cases nor a general clinical necessity test follows by contraposition. A different surrounding formulation must be checked anew; these are not blanket signatures to an unseen opinion.

The medical-information omission defense remains separate wherever material: physician proves by a preponderance a reasonable belief that furnishing information would severely adversely affect the patient's physical or mental health. It does not create an automatic duty to omit, excuse ordinary waiting, or erase independent emergency criteria. No direct part-join may silently discard that defense when applying a challenged disclosure.

## Isolated rejection of the rigid trimester timetable

Proposed public proposition: 'Roe's rigid trimester timetable no longer supplies categorical immunity from regulation in the first trimester.'

| Justice | Join and source | Objection/minimum revision | Movement |
|---|---|---|---|
| White | **Directly joins the negative proposition**, independently supported by W-Thornburgh and his frozen overruling position. He can join rejection of a restriction on regulation without joining the protected-choice foundation others preserve. Quill/Hilton illustrate proposition separation. | Avoid suggesting Roe historically barred literally every professional qualification or consent rule. Preferred precision: **'Roe's rigid trimester timetable no longer bars otherwise constitutional abortion regulation solely because it operates in the first trimester.'** This says nothing about which broader test makes regulation constitutional. | None; this is contained in the frozen broader rejection, with no retained-core join. |
| Scalia | **Directly joins**, supported by S-Webster/S-Ohio and frozen rejection of both trimester and viability constraints. | Same precision. Cannot join an attached sentence reaffirming protected choice or making the new negative rule depend on that reaffirmance. A separate paragraph/part must allow the actual isolated join. | None. No logical-subset claim is needed to invent a join; it is express. |
| Thomas | **Directly joins**, as a limited proposition contained in his fresh frozen rejection of Roe's machinery; own Quill/McCollum/Wright method permits limited proposition agreement. | Same precision and no false previous Thomas abortion position. Does not resolve his model uncertainty about broader precedent through a purported historical inference. | None; narrower direct join coexists with his separately stated foundational disagreement. |

This can produce an express proposition coalition if enough other Justices join it, but neither a general replacement standard nor its converse is entailed. It must not be counted as a join to a complete protected-core opinion. The recommended wording is a precision correction, not a requested substantive commitment change.

## One White-authored joint separate writing

**Compatible proposal:** a single White-authored opinion states these three Justices' rational-review approach, rejection of Roe's fundamental abortion entitlement, their disagreements on provision outcomes and the independent grounds/reservations accepted in their freezes. It contains no universal Salerno rule. Separate equality subparts distinguish White's actual tier adoption from Scalia/Thomas's assumption, while a common application gives their reasons. Privacy is separately analyzed at its bounded actual recipient/claimed-entitlement scope. A second Scalia opinion is not required if it adds no necessary distinct legal position.

State specific abortion source/reliance grounds; preserve independently applicable rights and medically necessary-care reservations. No movement if so limited. |
| Constitutional stare decisis | Direct join to reasoned correction of Roe despite acknowledged reliance, consistent with W-Thornburgh. | Direct join to error/source/workability and institution-sensitive distinction, consistent with Webster/Quill. | Direct join to the fresh balance already frozen, expressly compatible with exact adherence in McCollum/Quill. | No claim precedent automatically yields whenever wrong; no fictitious denial that reproductive expectations exist. No imported historical polemic or blanket rule that statutory and constitutional reliance are identical. No movement. |

Actual statutory exceptions must be stated. No movement. |
| Equality: applicability of intermediate scrutiny | **White-only adoption** of important-objective/substantial-relationship test, consistent with MichaelM/Caban actual joins. | **Refuses an actual-tier-adoption sentence**; assumes that test without deciding it. | **Refuses actual-tier-adoption sentence**; same assumption, with stated absence of prior abortion/equality writing. | Isolate the tier-selection sentence/part as White alone; or write a common merits application preceded by a clear attribution of White's application and Scalia/Thomas's assumption. Do not silently turn assumptions into a Court-wide tier holding or White's definite choice into an unqualified refusal to decide. No substantive movement if topology preserves distinction. |

No biological-label shortcut, universal maternal/paternal stereotype or general male authority. No movement. |
| Independent marital/informational privacy | Direct join to rejection of the specifically claimed facial immunity on recipients/data before the Court, while keeping actual privacy/confidentiality law. | Direct join to this bounded proposition without all-encompassing family-control theory. | Direct join at bounded claim/source scope. | Do not erase Griswold/Eisenstadt, authorize public patient identities or conflate notice privacy with independent speech. No movement. |
| Separate spousal-speech reach and emergency-vagueness reach | Direct join to frozen nonmerits/preservation reservations. | Direct join; no invented waiver or paid/private-speech shortcut. | Direct join; own Riggins/Wright scope discipline without invented concession. | A common writing cannot announce that every constitutional ground fails. Reversal is on adjudicated grounds; ordinary procedure can determine genuinely preserved live theories. No standalone vagueness merits holding. No movement. |
| Remaining provision disagreements and remedy | Direct join where actual frozen specific grounds are retained. | Same. | Same. | Item-by-item scope, actual statutory terms and conditional-report reservation remain. A direct join to a separate common permission elsewhere does not make dissent from a different invalidated provision inconsistent. No movement absent a new remedy/formulation. |

**Authorship and assignment:** White authorship is compatible with all three accepted grounds and requires no substantive change. If this separate-writing coalition consists of White, Scalia and Thomas and the Chief is not part of it, White is its senior participating Associate Justice and may assign its common writing to himself under§8. This is a conditional authority determination on the supplied coalition, not an invented historical assignment. Root must state the actual coalition and assignment basis at assembly. If the Chief participates in that coalition, the actual assignment rule must instead be applied to that fact.

**No necessary second Scalia writing:** Scalia's distinct direct-source emphasis can be expressed within the common abortion-specific reasoning without White or Thomas adopting propositions they reject. His Riley constraints, nonjoin to an actual equality-tier adoption and lack of a universal Salerno commitment are preserved by the part topology. If the eventual White text adds broader substantive-due-process or stare-decisis claims that one refuses, first test a narrower revision/partial join; a separate writing is needed only for a real remaining published legal distinction, not because history contained one.

## Audit conclusion

The exact revised physician-delivery/waiting rationale supports **three direct part-joins** from White, Scalia and Thomas at the sufficient-permission level, with the stated contextual qualifications. Exact common clinical/content/speech/report permissions can likewise attract direct joins despite disagreement about the broader abortion foundation. All three can expressly join the isolated negative trimester proposition. A White-authored common separate writing is compatible with the frozen framework and provision positions if equality-tier attribution, independent grounds, reach reservations and report-remedy conditions are preserved.

**No substantive non-Stone commitment has moved in this audit.** Formal joins to newly specified compatible parts are assembly determinations; they do not adopt a comprehensive framework or change primary judgments. Any insertion of an unstated threshold, a new constitutional foundation, a categorical Salerno rule, a new factual premise or an untested conditional remedy must return through the appropriate reconciliation check. This audit is not approval of an unseen full opinion.

### Diagnostic-report precise corrections

The statute distinguishes **what the physician must do to determine age** from **what the physician reports having actually done**. The incorporated statutory text says the physician reports the types of inquiries made, examinations/tests utilized, and the basis for the diagnosis. The preceding clinical assessment uses what a prudent physician considers necessary. The current rule's 'actual inquiries, examinations and tests a reasonably prudent physician would consider necessary' blurs those commands and could imply that a test actually used need not be reported if deemed unnecessary. No frozen Justice selected that interpretation.

Recommended rule language:

> Section3214(a)(11) may require the types of inquiries made and examinations and tests utilized, and the basis for the gestational-age diagnosis, as described in§3210(a). The underlying assessment calls for the inquiries, examinations and tests a prudent physician would consider necessary; a medical emergency preventing compliance supplies the statutory exception. The reporting field is distinct from§3214(a)(5)'s age result and imposes no fixed additional test.

That entry should itself carry the source and actual§3203 emergency terms, or include the common definition within the same holding entry: good-faith clinical judgment necessitating immediate abortion to avert death or delay creating serious risk of substantial and irreversible major-function impairment. The existence of a medical emergency alone is not silently substituted for§3210(a)'s additional **prevents compliance** condition.

The sentence **'Webster's treatment of medically appropriate assessment does not authorize useless procedures'** should remain a statement of what this report commands, not a new general constitutional test. Recommended:

> Webster does not supply a fixed testing command absent from this statute. The present reporting permission follows§3210(a)'s actual prudent-physician standard and does not itself require an additional examination or test.

All three accept that narrower source explanation directly. If instead the text means every otherwise lawful legislative testing requirement is unconstitutional unless a court finds it medically useful in an individual case, all three refuse that added rule; it exceeds the freeze and would require substantive reconciliation. The suggested revision avoids that unadopted proposition while preserving the actual permission.

### Notice-report topology

Root clarified that the whole notice-invalidity/severance part has its own five direct signers; White/Scalia/Thomas have no conditional votes. That treatment matches the freeze. Their existing primary judgment sustains all three fields, including marital status, on an intact-report premise. No new separate joinder to the complete severance rationale is necessary or supplied here. If the final topology lists their support for preserving marital-status collection as a judgment/proposition support, it must identify that **primary sustention ground**, not pretend they voted conditionally to remove the notice fields or joined an opinion premised on their invalidity.

These three are not new signers merely because they agree no majority formula can be invented. |

### Preserved audit material — final join oconnor kennedy

# Final O’Connor–Kennedy join and assignment audit

**Matter:** Casey, Nos. 91-744 and 91-902, effective June 29, 1992. **Research cutoff:** June 29, 1992. **Input:** decision-data.json, 40 actual opinion parts, 27 holdings, 31 judgment rows and six separate-writing summaries. The data bytes used to enumerate the final audit rows have SHA-256 6bcc904e4e20c58f8c2f561bc1bad5cf0e67af54d98a35f6860e37cb737d2567. Root subsequently authorized the isolated White-tier correction described below; root controls rebuilding the data. This audit writes only scratch files and changes no committed artifact.

**Stage and scope.** This is an actual compatibility test after the all-eight freeze, historical reconciliation and authorized opening of Stone v1.2. It does not claim blindness or invent circulation, dialogue or draft history. The controlling inputs are provisional-oconnor-kennedy.md, reconciliation-oconnor-kennedy.md and compatibility-oconnor-kennedy.md, together with the frozen neutral source distinctions. The assembled holdings, all six summaries and all 40 parts were examined; earlier truncations were recovered by bounded reads or byte comparisons to the fully read draft holdings. No new source research or new substantive commitment was used.

## Result and exact corrections

1. Every Court-part join attributed to these two Justices is compatible at the stated scope. All Chief/White assignments follow the actual judgment coalition and seniority. The isolated trimester proposition has its own six, distinct from the protected-choice six. Delivery and waiting have five **direct** sufficient-ground joins; their accepted findings and express absence of severe access restriction avoid substituting total impossibility for O’Connor’s threshold. Kennedy joins those particular permissions without adopting her comprehensive formula.
2. **Required join correction, accepted by root:** add O’Connor to the isolated White-tier part. It selects intermediate scrutiny separately from White’s sustaining equality application. Hogan makes her refusal of that isolated selection unsupported. The tier proposition has White and O’Connor (two); Scalia and Thomas assume it without deciding; Kennedy reserves it. White’s sustaining application retains White/Scalia/Thomas, while O’Connor’s independent invalidating equality application remains hers alone. This changes a compatible part joinder, not a frozen legal commitment; no §8 substantive-movement audit is triggered.
3. **Source-completeness correction:** the medical-necessity-report holding should expressly state the 24-or-more-weeks predicate of the §3211(b)(1) obligation it identifies by reference. That is a source precision fix, not a change to either Justice’s sustaining conclusion. Root was notified.
4. The narrow eight-Justice materials and attendance grounds remain compatible. They accept actual findings, preserve live application/construction questions, certify no universal factual accuracy, and announce no rule that bypass cures every needless visit for an already-consenting parent. Narrowing within the frozen available grounds does not move either commitment.
5. The notice-report part correctly has five full reasoned joins: Blackmun, Stevens, O’Connor, Kennedy and Souter. Stone additionally joins notice-field invalidation, producing six for that proposition, and joins marital-status retention without a ground under his silent-component fallback. White/Scalia/Thomas sustain the status field on their intact-report premise, not a fictitious conditional severance vote. The Chief may assign because the assignment rule follows judgment participation, not only full-part rationale participation.
6. The dedicated governing-standard holding is expressly a composite statement of the actual signed holdings, not a new unanimous or six-Justice framework writing. These two accept its accurate account of authority boundaries. The description of Stone’s meaningful-burden approach, O’Connor’s possible compelling justification, Kennedy’s concrete-only commitments and the lack of a logical-subset majority does not attribute to them another Justice’s framework.

## Source key

Each table cell rests first on the Justice’s already fully read date-eligible writing. OA: O’Connor, Akron dissent, 462 U.S. 416, 452–475. OT: O’Connor, Thornburgh dissent, 476 U.S. 747, 814–832. OW: O’Connor, Webster concurrence, 492 U.S. 490, 522–531, and her actual Court joins. OH/OHS: O’Connor’s Hodgson concurrence, 497 U.S. 417, 458–461, and joined Stevens Parts I, II, IV–VII; not III or VIII. OG: O’Connor’s Hogan opinion, 458 U.S. 718. OM/KM: O’Connor concurrence joined Kennedy in Michael H., 491 U.S. 110, 132. KW: Webster Court/plurality writing joined Kennedy, 492 U.S. 490, especially 518–522 and note 15. KH: Kennedy’s Hodgson writing, 497 U.S. 417, 480–497. KO: Kennedy’s Ohio v. Akron Center opinion, 497 U.S. 502; O’Connor did not join Part V. KP: Souter’s Payne concurrence joined Kennedy, 501 U.S. 808, 842–856. KJ: Kennedy’s Texas v. Johnson concurrence, 491 U.S. 397, 420–421.

The actual simulated public continuity references, fully specified in entering_law.md and the frozen annex, remain limited to their own settings: R = Riggins/Foucha in output chunk 9; L = Lee in chunk 13; M = Medina in chunk 13; C = Collins in chunk 5; Q/H = Quill in chunk 9 and Hilton in chunk 3 (O’Connor dissented in Hilton; Kennedy authored); X = Hudson in chunk 5 and Wright in chunk 12. They support attention to the concrete liberty, evidence and authority level, not invented earlier abortion positions. Historical Casey confirms or exposes departures in the separate reconciliation annex; it supplies no new position here.

## All 40 actual parts: judgment, ground, objection, minimum revision and assignment

“JOIN” means a direct join to the actual bounded formulation. “REFUSE/NONREACH” identifies a reserved independent ground and does not invent a contrary merits vote. Author self-joins count in the topology. A minimum revision that changes Stone’s stated view is only a compatibility boundary, not authority to rewrite his approved position.

| Actual opinion part | O’Connor | Kennedy | Assignment and basis |
|---|---|---|---|
| The framework retaining protected choice (protected-choice) | JOIN. OA/OT/OW and OM preserve the protected decision, the viability boundary and necessary life/health care without making every burden forbidden. R/C/X require the identified liberty and distinguish disposition from a complete test. Objection: none to this bounded text. Minimum revision: none; do not add the rejected universal Step 5 burden rule. | JOIN. ## Public summaries, nonreach and final audit boundary

O’Connor’s full separate summary preserves her exact initial threshold (absolute obstacle or severe limitation), no weighing of State interests at that first step, possible compelling justification afterward, and rational review below. It accurately states her independent Hogan equality and narrow adult reproductive-communication grounds without turning them into a Court holding. Kennedy’s refusal of her complete concurrence does not contradict his joins to its common dispositions: he has selected neither that universal severity scale nor those additional independent merits grounds. Both reserve independent spousal compelled speech and standalone emergency vagueness, with preservation left unresolved rather than called waived.

The six published summaries and parts accurately distinguish their disagreement with Stone’s full burden/fit formula from agreement with its analytic identification, factual causation and limited clinical-report propositions. Their refusal of Stone’s particular invalidations and focused remedies rests on the same grounds frozen before Stone was opened. The reasoned-majority medical/report permissions cannot be recharacterized as an adoption of Stone’s universal clinical-consensus or State-proof rules. No historical authorship, joint opinion or automatic general substantial-obstacle safe harbor is imported.

The exact source-completeness correction and isolated tier-join correction above exhaust this audit’s requested changes. Any later change to the public formulation must preserve the identified limits or receive a new compatibility test; a changed substantive non-Stone commitment still requires the Engine’s sanitized reconciliation procedure.

### Preserved audit material — final join blackmun stevens souter

# Final section 8 join audit — Blackmun, Stevens and Souter

## Scope and result

Reviewed `decision-data.json`: all 40 proposed opinion-part descriptors, all 27 operative holding blocks (including rules, explanations, source qualifications, precedent treatment and authority), every judgment row, and all six separate-writing summaries. The initially clipped trimester/emergency output was recovered by a dedicated full read. This is an audit of the actual supplied formulations and summaries; no unprovided full opinion, conversation or drafting history is invented.

**Result: the proposed B/Stevens/Souter part joins and judgment memberships are consistent with their frozen and comparator-reconciled commitments, at the precise limits below. No commitment moves.** Their acknowledgment of the composite governing-standard statement is not an operative joinder in provisions or timetable changes they oppose. Clinical speech and ancillary speech have the clarified frozen scope recorded in `compatibility-blackmun-stevens-souter.md`; this is not a new post-Stone ground.

Codes: **J** = direct join at the part's expressly bounded scope; **R** = refusal of that part; **P** = only the expressly identified severed proposition is compatible and no full-part join is counted. **R—unreached** means declining an unnecessary independent merits ground, not a merits vote against the claim. An author is counted among the joins to the author's own part. “Minimum revision” identifies what another formulation would need; it does not change Stone's approved text or any Justice's commitment.

Own-source keys, all date-eligible: BR Blackmun's Roe (1973); BT Blackmun's Thornburgh (1986), joined Stevens; BA Blackmun's Ashcroft (1983), joined Stevens; BW Blackmun's Webster (1989); BO Blackmun's Ohio v. Akron Center (1990); ST Stevens's Thornburgh (1986); SW Stevens's Webster (1989); SH Stevens's Hodgson (1990); SO Stevens's Ohio v. Akron Center (1990), joining I–IV but not V; SR Stevens's Riley joins (1988); SP Souter's Payne concurrence (June 27, 1991). Souter had no date-eligible abortion writing. Only each Justice's actual joined public proposition is used, as cataloged in the frozen source matrix.

## Assignment, aggregate authority and remaining copy checks

Assignments to Stevens for the parts where Stone is in the actual judgment coalition, and to O'Connor by White where Stone opposes that judgment, satisfy the stated seniority rule for these coalitions. The timetable part can be assigned by Stone because he joins that isolated judgment. Stone's minority parts and each separate writer's own opinion require no invented majority assignment. This audit confirms compatibility; it does not import historical authorship. No author choice changes the legal grounds or joins.

The notice-report part has five direct full-part joins including all three audited Justices. Stone's sixth direct notice-invalidity vote and fallback marital-status retention are separately stated. White/Scalia/Thomas's primary intact-report votes supply no conditional severance vote. The receipt part's nine direct common documentation joins coexist with eight ancillary links and five timing/delivery links; no full nine-Justice approval of all links is inferred. The governing-standard block is expressly composite, not a separately signed majority formula. These distinctions reconcile the proposed topology to the judgment table.

Three source/presentation refinements were sent to root without any vote change:

1. The spouse statutory capsule's “qualifying threat of compulsion” should give the incorporated §3128 words: **“by threat of forcible compulsion that would prevent resistance by a person of reasonable resolution”** (district finding 268; reproduced in the neutral packet). The distinct district finding 297 account supplies the 90-day reporting restriction. Preserve those source classifications.
2. In Blackmun's public summary, “no ... compelled-speech holding” in the spouse paragraph should read **“no ... spousal compelled-speech holding”** so it cannot contradict the separate clinical-disclosure permission. The local `separate-bss.json` is corrected.
3. Directory defects belong to **district findings 181–184**; findings 185–189 concern individualized informed consent. Blackmun/Stevens summary references were narrowed locally to 181–184. Their substantive observations are unchanged. Local counts after the correction are Blackmun 461, Stevens 471 and Souter 478 words.

Scratch-only source-drafting `authority_note` fields containing “proposed,” “confirm” or references to a historical framework must not be published as if they were part of the Court's reasoning. The final `authority` fields supply the actual named coalition. This is a presentation filter, not a change in holdings.

No unresolved B/Stevens/Souter vote or joinder blocker remains once the stated scopes and source wording are retained. Any subsequent change in an actual part's ground or limits requires a renewed part-specific check; this audit does not approve unseen broader text.

### Preserved audit material — final join white scalia thomas

# Casey v1.2 — final White / Scalia / Thomas joinder and judgment audit

## Result and boundary

All 40 proposed public opinion parts and all 31 judgment rows in decision-data.json have been individually checked against these three Justices’ frozen positions and the actual public reasons. Their listed joins/refusals reconcile. No non-Stone commitment moves. The listed direct joins are substantive adoption of those exact narrow grounds, not votes inferred from the disposition or an “undue burden” label. Three judgment votes for the parental structure do not join constitutional bypass necessity; primary sustention of marital data does not become a conditional notice-report severance vote.

This audit incorporates the exact minimal common-wording repairs in final-common-wording-fixes.json. Root must rebuild decision-data.json from those corrected scratch drafts before freezing production. The corrections remove unintended general necessity/usefulness premises; they change no vote, legal outcome or remedy. Other Justices’ joins, assignment choices and final production topology remain root’s responsibility. The Chief/senior-associate assignment bases listed in the reviewed public data conflict with none of these three Justices’ actual component memberships; no historical authorship is imported.

Only sanitized proposed public Stone reasoning was read, after the global freeze and comparator reconciliation. The private Stone supplement and old simulated Casey record remain unopened. This is the disclosed single-conversation mode, not true blindness. Historical comparison had already confirmed the primary positions; it does not supply any join here. Thomas has no date-eligible abortion writing, and his bounded fresh conclusion does not erase the unresolved prefreeze question whether reliance and adherence should prevail.

## Source keys used in every row

These keys identify the Justice’s own date-eligible writings and actual simulated earlier-term public positions, not historical Casey opinions. Full source reads and exact prefreeze component citations are in white_scalia_thomas_authority_notes.md, provisional-white-scalia-thomas.md and entering_law.md. The published earlier positions are in terms/OT1991/output/ at the cited chunks. Research cutoff June 29, 1992.

- **W1:** White’s Doe v. Bolton dissent addressing Roe, 410 U.S. 179, 221–223 (1973); his Danforth separate opinion, 428 U.S. 52, 92–101 (1976); Thornburgh dissent, 476 U.S. 747, 786–814 (1986). Elective-choice priority, actual parental interest, source, burden and precedent arguments; Doe expressly reserves unpresented medical-necessity issues.
- **W2:** White’s Danforth and Thornburgh writings on accurate information, medical construction, parental alternatives, actual trial findings, reporting, harassment and severance. Public continuity: Foucha, May 18 (chunk 9), addresses a continuing lawful basis for confinement; Wright, June 19 (chunk 12), expressly accepts record-specific sufficiency without joining a whole theory; Medina, June 22 (chunk 13), uses its particular procedural source. None supplies an automatic abortion outcome.
- **W3:** White’s Caban opinion, 441 U.S. 380 (1979), and his actual join in Brennan’s Michael M. dissent, 450 U.S. 464, 488–496 (1981), for important objective/substantial relationship; his Riley majority join, 487 U.S. 781 (1988), for independent speech. Earlier-term Foucha and Lee positions remain source-specific; Lee (June 24, chunk 13) does not erase other forms of constitutional compulsion.
- **W4:** White’s Thornburgh precedent analysis and actual Quill positions, May 26 (chunk 9): due-process revision accepted but dormant-Commerce adherence rejected. Proposition and institutional setting govern; constitutional error is not insulated merely by renewed disagreement.
- **S1:** Scalia’s Webster concurrence, 492 U.S. 490, 532–537 (1989), and Ohio v. Akron Center concurrence, 497 U.S. 502, 520–521 (1990). Express Roe objection coexists with direct joinder of genuinely compatible narrow parts.
- **S2:** Scalia’s Riley majority join except footnote 11 and concurrence, 487 U.S. 781, 803–804 (1988), plus S1. Compensation or truth alone does not validate all compelled expression. His actual Wright and Riggins positions (chunks 12 and 9) require attention to the particular legal claim and record.
- **S3:** Scalia’s Michael H. opinion, 491 U.S. 110 (1989), supplies a method for identifying a particular claimed liberty, not the sex-classification tier. His actual Riley, Riggins, Medina and Lee writings/joins preserve source-specific treatment. Caban’s sex-classification standard is expressly assumed here without deciding applicability; no imaginary Scalia abortion-equality opinion is supplied.
- **S4:** Scalia’s actual Quill concurrence, May 26 (chunk 9), and McCollum dissent, June 18 (chunk 12), distinguish adherence, extension, source and congressional correction; S1 supplies his abortion-specific reason for a different result.
- **T1:** Thomas’s own Hudson dissent (February 25, chunk 5), Riggins dissent (May 18, chunk 9), Wright principal opinion (June 19, chunk 12), and actual Collins/Foucha/Medina/Lee joins provide bounded source/history/record methods. They are not abortion writings. The prefreeze Roe-adherence alternative remains explicit.
- **T2:** Thomas’s Wright opinion and Medina join, together with Riggins’s claim/remedy distinctions and Collins’s source-limited substantive-due-process rule. These support exact statutory predicates and scope rather than a borrowed universal burden formula.
- **T3:** Thomas has no own pre-cutoff abortion-speech or spousal-equality holding. His actual Riggins, Collins, Medina and Lee positions demand claim-specific treatment, assessed against existing Riley/Wooley speech law and Caban’s expressly assumed intermediate sex-classification standard. His uncertainty is bounded by those actual sources, not filled by historical Casey’s outcome.
- **T4:** Thomas’s actual Quill concurrence join and McCollum concurrence in judgment demonstrate adherence despite disagreement with a premise; T1 supplies the competing source/history path. His frozen reconsideration conclusion is fresh and bounded, not a preexisting abortion position.

## Every actual opinion part

“Join” includes an author’s own part; “refuse” means no formal join to the complete part. Compatible isolated descriptive propositions inside a refused writing are identified but not added to its count. All entries have **no movement** from the final frozen commitments and sanitized compatibility checks.

## Framework subpropositions within the refused complete proposal

The Stone Steps 1–5 part is not rejected merely because of its name. White, Scalia and Thomas can identify the actual obligation and operation (Step 1), consider objective proof rather than assumed motives (Step 2), define the genuinely affected population without circularly defining it by ultimate invalidity (Step 3), and evaluate real causal/cumulative effects without attributing all preexisting hardship to the State (Step 4). Those descriptive disciplines do not make any of them join an abortion-liberty purpose prohibition, an independently fatal substantial obstacle to protected previability care, or the meaningful-burden/State-justification and alternatives requirements in Step 5. Their own W1/W2, S1 and T1/T2 bases remain different. Step 6’s large-fraction qualification likewise receives no formal join from them; rejecting it is not adoption of universal Salerno. Clinical limits are separated in the row above from their already adopted statutory after-care reporting rationale. Thus no partial methodological overlap is silently counted as adoption of either a full step containing a disputed constitutional premise or a complete general framework.

## Independent reach, narrow grounds and assignment checks

- These three’s spousal-notice sustention does not adjudicate the distinct spouse-compelled-speech merits. The argument did raise that theory. Their writing leaves its genuinely preserved scope and ordinary disposition for proper handling; neither a fictional waiver nor the other six Justices’ liberty invalidation is used as their sufficient ground. Emergency health construction likewise does not decide standalone vagueness or disputed preservation.
- The adult-incapacity guardian clause and unappealed collection/ascertainment questions are not silently added to any judgment or opinion coalition. Parent information is a lower-court content construction, not express wholesale incorporation of every §3205 delivery/timing mechanism. Every exact statutory qualification remains supplied in its holding and source packet.
- White authors the common separate writing without a majority-assignment claim. Scalia and Thomas join its actual intermediate-scrutiny application on an assumption, not White’s selected tier. No second Scalia writing is necessary to preserve a missing position, and none is manufactured.
- Where White assigns O’Connor a Court permission because the Chief dissents, White actually belongs to that component’s judgment coalition and is the senior participating Associate Justice. Chief assignments in components he supports are not displaced by White’s seniority. This checks eligibility/basis only; the choice of O’Connor or Stevens as author is a simulated public assignment, not a recovered historical fact.
- The clinical and parent wording corrections remove unintended joins to broad premises that their prefreeze rows rejected. They narrow common opinion language to already frozen grounds; they do not change a Justice’s position. Section 8’s movement reconciliation is therefore not triggered by those edits. Any later addition of a protected-core, universal speech, universal Salerno, constitutional clinical-usefulness, or conditional reporting premise would require a fresh audit rather than inheriting this approval.
